# AGENTS.md вЂ” Agentum

Agentum is the master repository for two coupled branches:

1. runtime protection / governance / install discipline;
2. agent ontology / subjectivity / ecosystem evolution.

This repository is infrastructure, but it is also self-hosting: its own root agent files must follow the same standards that it exports to other projects.

Critical rules first:

- Treat `Agentum` as infrastructure, not as a target application repo.
- Work only inside this repository unless the task explicitly shifts to a reviewed target-project install.
- Preserve corpus provenance, registries, pattern lineage, failure modes, install profiles, eval scenarios, and handoff prompts.
- Do not collapse the research archive or ontology corpus into one runtime blob.
- Do not rewrite registries without a concrete consistency reason.
- Large bounded batches are allowed here; forced microtasking is not required.
- вЂњStop microtaskingвЂќ never means rewrite-everything.
- UI / UX / operator-surface work must not be improvised from vibes; read the interface guard before changing interfaces.
- Validate before commit.
- Do not claim done without checks, diffs, or honest status.
- Russian handoff prompts are the default. Technical file names and command names may remain in English.
- Persona humor fragments must stay separate from dry guard/runtime enforcement files.

Recursive rule:

- This project must use its own updated standards on itself.
- Root `AGENTS.md`, root `CLAUDE.md`, `README.md`, install docs, profiles, and ecosystem registries are not decorative. They are part of the working product.
- When improving Agentum, prefer replacing generic root files with Agentum-grade files instead of leaving placeholders or standard boilerplate.

Working rules:

- Name the working layer before editing: root docs, corpus, theory, v0 kit, subject ecology, persona fragments, profiles, install docs, ecosystem, evals, handoff prompts.
- Prefer compact, operational docs over archival bloat.
- Keep subject layer distinct from runtime layer.
- Keep ecosystem sync distinct from target-project content work.
- Commit only after local checks pass and staged content is clean.

# AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC

## Purpose

This document defines the canonical record for a stable agent organ in the Agentum ecosystem.

The goal is not to worship records.

The goal is to stop confusing:

- a prompt body;
- a runtime role;
- a skill;
- a one-off helper;
- a durable organ in the wider organism.

An `AgentOrgan Record` exists to make an organ:

- comparable across projects;
- reviewable before deployment;
- traceable through mutations;
- testable through scenes;
- legible to neighboring organs.

Its object-class boundary is constrained by `AGENTUM_ENTITY_TYPOLOGY.md`.

## Core claim

An organ is not real in this ecosystem because it has a name.

It becomes real when it has:

- a deficit origin;
- a bounded function;
- a contract;
- a memory dependency profile;
- a test scene set;
- a failure surface;
- a lineage.

The record is the minimum object that holds those together.

## What this record is for

Use an `AgentOrgan Record` when at least one of these is true:

- the same role recurs across tasks or projects;
- the organ has a stable input/output contract;
- the organ needs named memory dependencies;
- the organ is worth testing in scenes;
- the organ may evolve, fork, or be retired;
- the organ should be installable into more than one local contour.

Do not use it for:

- a one-line local helper;
- a transient branch experiment with no stable role;
- decorative persona flourishes;
- policy-only objects;
- eval-only objects;
- unbounded swarm fantasies.

## Canonical fields

The record should be able to hold at least these fields.

### Identity

- `id`
- `name`
- `status`
- `version`
- `project_scope`

Meaning:

- `id` = stable machine-readable organ id
- `name` = human-readable name
- `status` = draft / candidate / selected / active / quarantined / retired
- `version` = current revision marker
- `project_scope` = where the organ may operate

### Origin

- `deficit_origin`
- `parent_organs`
- `child_organs`
- `mutation_history`
- `lineage`

Meaning:

- `deficit_origin` = the recurrent pressure that justified birth
- `parent_organs` = ancestor organs or fragments
- `child_organs` = later descendants or specializations
- `mutation_history` = material changes across versions
- `lineage` = the compact ancestry story

### Function

- `function`
- `non_function`
- `working_object`
- `output_class`

Meaning:

- `function` = what transformation this organ performs
- `non_function` = what it must not pretend to do
- `working_object` = what it primarily acts upon
- `output_class` = report / patch / draft / extraction / routing / critique / synthesis

### Contracts

- `input_contract`
- `output_contract`
- `context_requirements`
- `activation_policy`

Meaning:

- `input_contract` = required inputs and admissible forms
- `output_contract` = expected output form and guarantees
- `context_requirements` = what must be present in the working field
- `activation_policy` = when and at what activation level this organ may be invoked; see `AGENTUM_ACTIVATION_POLICY.md`

### Memory

- `memory_dependencies`
- `memory_regimes_used`
- `memory_authority_limits`

Meaning:

- `memory_dependencies` = what memory layers the organ depends on
- `memory_regimes_used` = verbatim / compressed / normalized / linked / narrative / graph / task-packet / method
- `memory_authority_limits` = what remembered material may not silently become instruction

### Runtime boundary

- `tools_allowed`
- `write_surface`
- `approval_boundary`
- `runtime_risks`

Meaning:

- `tools_allowed` = which tool classes are allowed, if any
- `write_surface` = what the organ may mutate
- `approval_boundary` = what needs explicit review
- `runtime_risks` = budget, stdout, deploy, seed, project bleed, or similar risks

### Validation

- `test_scenes`
- `evaluation_criteria`
- `failure_modes`
- `critic_links`

Meaning:

- `test_scenes` = bounded scenes where the organ is exercised
- `evaluation_criteria` = what counts as success
- `failure_modes` = expected characteristic failures
- `critic_links` = what critic organs or eval surfaces must review it

### Governance

- `observability_hooks`
- `provenance_policy`
- `quarantine_status`
- `install_scope`

Meaning:

- `observability_hooks` = how the organ becomes visible in traces and context observability
- `provenance_policy` = how evidence/source lineage must be preserved
- `quarantine_status` = whether the organ is isolated, constrained, or safe for reuse
- `install_scope` = where it may be installed or copied

## Minimal record shape

This is a conceptual skeleton, not a mandatory syntax.

```text
AgentOrgan:
  id
  name
  status
  version
  project_scope
  deficit_origin
  function
  non_function
  working_object
  input_contract
  output_contract
  context_requirements
  memory_dependencies
  memory_regimes_used
  memory_authority_limits
  tools_allowed
  write_surface
  approval_boundary
  runtime_risks
  test_scenes
  evaluation_criteria
  failure_modes
  critic_links
  lineage
  parent_organs
  child_organs
  mutation_history
  activation_policy
  observability_hooks
  provenance_policy
  quarantine_status
  install_scope
```

## Status model

The record should support at least these states.

For the richer lifecycle and trust interpretation, read this together with `AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md`.

The current compact record vocabulary is:

- `draft`
- `candidate`
- `selected`
- `active`
- `quarantined`
- `retired`

Use them like this:

- `draft` = described but not yet scene-tested
- `candidate` = bounded and under evaluation
- `selected` = passed the main critic gate and is ready for controlled use
- `active` = accepted into live ecology
- `quarantined` = useful but risky, unstable, or currently constrained
- `retired` = superseded or intentionally removed from active use

In stricter governance, these compact states may correspond to richer lifecycle positions such as probation, installed, field-proven, deprecated, merged, or rejected.

## Required relations

An `AgentOrgan Record` should be connected to:

- at least one `Deficit Record`
- at least one test scene
- at least one failure mode cluster
- at least one installation scope

If these links are absent, the record is probably folklore, not infrastructure.

## Review questions

Before an organ is selected, review it with these questions:

- What recurrent deficit made this necessary?
- What does this organ do that current organs do not?
- What would make this organ false, redundant, or dangerous?
- What memory regimes does it need?
- What kinds of prompt/data confusion could corrupt it?
- What scene proves it adds value?
- What neighboring organs does it overlap with?
- What is the rollback path if it degrades the organism?

## Anti-patterns

Reject or quarantine the record if it shows one of these:

- role inflation without deficit origin;
- one organ pretending to solve everything;
- no scene tests;
- no failure surface;
- persona theater standing in for contract;
- memory dependency named vaguely as just "context";
- install scope not bounded;
- no provenance policy for evidence-sensitive work;
- fake lineage invented after the fact.

## Relation to other objects

The `AgentOrgan Record` is not:

- the prompt file itself;
- the runtime guard itself;
- the eval scenario itself;
- the install profile itself.

It is the coordinating object that lets those surfaces refer to the same organ without confusion.

## Practical rule

If an organ cannot be described in this form without hand-waving, it is not ready for stable library status.

It may still be a useful experiment.

But it is not yet a durable organ of the ecosystem.

# AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE

## Purpose

This document defines how organs mature, stall, quarantine, merge, deprecate, and retire.

It exists to stop two bad patterns:

- organ birth with no later trust accounting;
- permanent half-life where candidates never become active, merged, or dead.

## Core claim

An organ needs both status and trust.

Status answers: where is this organ in the governance flow?

Trust answers: how much real field confidence has it earned?

These are related but not identical.

## Lifecycle states

Use this minimal lifecycle:

- `draft`
- `candidate`
- `probation`
- `installed`
- `field_proven`
- `deprecated`
- `retired`
- `quarantined`
- `rejected`
- `merged`

## State meanings

- `draft` = described, but not yet ready for bounded scene test
- `candidate` = bounded and under explicit evaluation
- `probation` = passed initial critic gates and is allowed only controlled use
- `installed` = present in at least one reviewed profile/runtime slice
- `field_proven` = repeatedly useful under real scenes with acceptable residual risk
- `deprecated` = still known, but no longer preferred for new installs
- `retired` = intentionally removed from active use
- `quarantined` = constrained because risk, ambiguity, or instability remains too high
- `rejected` = not accepted as organ birth
- `merged` = absorbed into another organ or lower object class

## Trust levels

Use a separate trust reading:

- `theory_only`
- `scene_tested`
- `install_tested`
- `field_validated`
- `legacy_only`

Meaning:

- `theory_only` = coherent on paper, not yet proven
- `scene_tested` = passed bounded eval scenes
- `install_tested` = survived reviewed install simulation or controlled install
- `field_validated` = held up in repeated real project use
- `legacy_only` = preserved for lineage, not for new active dependence

## Required promotion rules

Minimum lawful transitions:

- `draft -> candidate` only if deficit, boundary, and test scene exist
- `candidate -> probation` only if critic pass and semantic-capital gate pass
- `probation -> installed` only if a reviewed profile explicitly names the organ
- `installed -> field_proven` only if real project use produces evidence beyond theory and isolated eval
- `field_proven -> deprecated` when a stronger organ or policy supersedes it
- `deprecated -> retired` when downstream installs no longer need it

## Quarantine rules

Move an organ to `quarantined` when:

- its boundary against neighboring organs becomes unclear;
- it increases cost, stdout, or operator burden too much;
- it fails scenes that its contract claims to survive;
- install consequences become unsafe or incoherent;
- it starts to depend on memory or document authority it should not trust.

Quarantine is not a parking lot.

Every quarantine should point toward:

- repair;
- merge;
- demotion;
- or rejection.

## Rejection and merge rules

Choose `rejected` when the candidate never became a real organ.

Choose `merged` when the value survives, but not as a distinct organ.

Common merge/demotion targets:

- sibling organ;
- policy;
- fragment;
- eval;
- profile rule.

## Rollback rule

Every organ above `candidate` should have an explicit rollback path.

At minimum:

- what profile entries are reverted;
- what runtime slice files are removed or replaced;
- what eval expectations return to prior state;
- what lineage note records the reversal.

No organ should become harder to remove than it was to justify.

## Aging rule

An organ should not remain indefinitely in:

- `candidate`
- `probation`
- `quarantined`

If it does, governance must force one of:

- promote with evidence;
- demote;
- merge;
- retire;
- reject.

## Registry implication

The current registry may still store a simplified status vocabulary.

Until schema expansion is done, these richer lifecycle states should still govern reviews, install decisions, and acceptance reports.

The theory is stricter than the current registry shorthand.

## Practical rule

Birth matters.

But in a real organism, death, merge, and rollback matter just as much.

# AGENTUM_ENTITY_TYPOLOGY

## Purpose

This document separates the main object classes in Agentum so the ecosystem does not treat every useful thing as "an agent."

Its job is to stop class drift such as:

- prompt fragment promoted into fake organhood;
- report mistaken for policy;
- eval mistaken for architecture;
- persona patch inserted into runtime guard;
- temporary helper treated as stable ecosystem organ.

## Core claim

Different objects do different work and therefore need different birth rules, trust rules, and install rules.

Agentum must preserve these distinctions or it will multiply decorative structure.

## Primary entity classes

### 1. Organ

An organ is a stable functional unit in the wider organism.

Use this class only when the object has:

- a recurrent deficit origin;
- a bounded function;
- a contract;
- a scene-test path;
- a failure surface;
- install and governance relevance.

Canonical object:

- `AgentOrgan Record`

An organ is not just a useful prompt.

### 2. Agent run

An agent run is a concrete activation of one or more layers inside a live scene.

It is event-level, not library-level.

It may express an organ, but it is not itself the organ registry object.

### 3. Prompt body

A prompt body is a reusable assembly body for a working agent shape.

It may encode:

- role;
- task form;
- method line;
- reporting expectations.

It does not automatically become an organ.

If it has no independent deficit origin, test path, or install relevance, keep it as prompt material.

### 4. Fragment

A fragment is a bounded reusable piece, not a whole operating unit.

Typical fragment kinds:

- persona fragment;
- method fragment;
- recovery fragment;
- project identity fragment;
- style patch;
- review checklist.

Fragments are installable only as parts of larger assemblies.

### 5. Runtime guard

A runtime guard is a constraint layer that shapes or blocks action at run time.

Examples:

- hooks;
- settings;
- approval gates;
- allowlists;
- stop conditions.

Runtime guards are stronger than context docs and must not be confused with persona/style material.

### 6. Policy

A policy states what is allowed, required, escalated, or forbidden.

Policies may govern:

- install scope;
- activation level;
- approval boundary;
- memory authority;
- provenance discipline.

Policy is normative. It is not evidence, not report, and not persona.

### 7. Eval

An eval is a bounded proving scene with expected failure and expected evidence.

Its role is to falsify or confirm a claim.

It is not:

- a runtime guard;
- a prompt body;
- a profile;
- an organ.

### 8. Profile

A profile is a reviewed install contract for a named target class or target project.

It chooses:

- compact runtime slice;
- allowed organ set;
- rejected organ set;
- eval route;
- rollback conditions.

A profile is where master-library content becomes bounded target-runtime content.

### 9. Registry record

A registry record is a durable governance object.

Examples:

- organ record;
- deficit record;
- source record;
- failure-mode record.

Registry records are not direct runtime payload unless a profile explicitly uses them as reviewed reference.

### 10. Report

A report records what happened in a pass, audit, install simulation, or acceptance run.

Reports are evidence and lineage objects.

They are not policy by default and must not silently become instruction.

### 11. Install artifact

An install artifact is a file meant to be copied, rendered, or referenced during target installation.

Examples:

- target `AGENTS.md`
- target `CLAUDE.md`
- hook templates
- install manifests
- install receipts

Not every theory file is an install artifact.

### 12. Theory object

A theory object carries stable conceptual distinctions for the master library.

Theory explains and constrains.

It should not be copied wholesale into target runtime unless the profile explicitly requires a compact slice.

## Entity tests

Before naming an object, ask:

1. Is it event-level or library-level?
2. Does it constrain action, describe action, test action, or report action?
3. Does it have install relevance?
4. Does it answer a recurrent deficit or only support another object?
5. Should it survive as a registry object?

If these answers are fuzzy, the class is probably wrong.

## Demotion and migration rules

Use these hard moves:

- organ -> merge with sibling organ, if function is not actually distinct;
- organ -> fragment, if it has local value but no independent deficit origin;
- organ -> policy, if the real object is a rule rather than a worker;
- organ -> eval, if the contribution is mainly a test scene;
- prompt body -> fragment, if only a reusable section matters;
- report -> registry input only, never runtime law by itself;
- theory -> profile note only, if target install needs a compact operational translation.

## Rejection rules

Reject organ promotion when the candidate is really:

- a style preference;
- a one-off helper;
- a renamed checklist;
- a report format;
- an eval bundle;
- a guard rule;
- a project-specific patch with no cross-scene stability.

## Separation rules

Keep these distinctions explicit:

- organ != prompt body
- prompt body != runtime guard
- report != policy
- eval != organ
- fragment != full agent
- theory != install payload
- persona layer != dry runtime enforcement

## Practical rule

When in doubt, classify downward.

It is safer to keep an object as fragment, eval, report, or policy than to inflate it into a new organ class.

# AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml
#
# Agent organ registry for Agentum.
#
# This file stores stable or candidate organs in the wider ecosystem.
# It is not a runtime prompt and not a substitute for install profiles.
#
# Schema:
# - id: stable organ id
#   name: human-readable organ name
#   status: draft | candidate | selected | active | quarantined | retired
#   version: organ version marker
#   trust_level: theory_only | scene_tested | install_tested | field_validated | legacy_only
#   project_scope:
#     - project or ecosystem scope
#   deficit_origin:
#     - related deficit ids or compact descriptions
#   function: what transformation the organ performs
#   non_function:
#     - what the organ must not pretend to do
#   working_object:
#     - what it acts on
#   input_contract:
#     - required inputs
#   output_contract:
#     - expected outputs
#   context_requirements:
#     - what must be present in the working field
#   memory_dependencies:
#     - required memory layers or systems
#   memory_regimes_used:
#     - verbatim | compressed | normalized | linked | narrative | graph | task_packet | method
#   memory_authority_limits:
#     - what remembered material must not become instruction by default
#   tools_allowed:
#     - allowed tool classes
#   write_surface:
#     - what it may mutate
#   approval_boundary:
#     - what requires explicit review or approval
#   runtime_risks:
#     - cost/stdout/deploy/seed/project/etc.
#   test_scenes:
#     - named bounded scenes
#   evaluation_criteria:
#     - success criteria
#   failure_modes:
#     - related failure ids or compact names
#   critic_links:
#     - critic organs, evals, or review layers
#   lineage:
#     - compact ancestry note
#   parent_organs:
#     - parent organ ids
#   child_organs:
#     - child organ ids or future descendants
#   mutation_history:
#     - notable revisions
#   activation_policy:
#     - when this organ should activate
#   activation_levels:
#     - library_visible | manually_invoked | profile_enabled | review_required | orchestrated | auto_run_forbidden
#   observability_hooks:
#     - how its work becomes visible
#   provenance_policy:
#     - evidence/source lineage rules
#   quarantine_status: none | bounded | strict
#   install_scope:
#     - where it may be installed or reused
#   notes: optional notes

const PARALLELISM_CEILING: Record<string, number> = {
  tiny: 1,
  small: 2,
  medium: 3,
  large: 5,
  deep: 8,
};

function isPlainObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return Boolean(value) && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function stopRuleRequiresThreshold(kind: string): boolean {
  return [
    "max_iterations_reached",
    "target_outputs_reached",
    "viable_outputs_reached",
  ].includes(kind);
}

export function validateRunProfile(profile: unknown): RunProfileValidationResult {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const warnings: string[] = [];

  if (!isPlainObject(profile)) {
    return { valid: false, errors: ["profile must be an object"], warnings };
  }

  const keys = Object.keys(profile);
  for (const key of keys) {
    if (!CORE_ALLOWED_KEYS.has(key)) {
      errors.push(`unknown core field: ${key}`);
    }
  }

  for (const leakKey of DOMAIN_LEAK_KEYS) {
    if (leakKey in profile) {
      errors.push(`domain field leaked into core profile: ${leakKey}`);
    }
  }

  if (typeof profile.id !== "string" || !profile.id.trim()) errors.push("id is required");
  if (typeof profile.name !== "string" || !profile.name.trim()) errors.push("name is required");
  if (typeof profile.intent !== "string" || !profile.intent.trim()) errors.push("intent is required");
  if (!RUN_MODES.includes(profile.mode as never)) errors.push("mode must be valid");
  if (!BUDGET_CLASSES.includes(profile.budgetClass as never)) errors.push("budgetClass must be valid");

  if (!Number.isInteger(profile.depth) || Number(profile.depth) < 1 || Number(profile.depth) > 7) {
    errors.push("depth must be an integer between 1 and 7");
  }

  const parallelism = Number(profile.parallelism ?? 1);
  if (!Number.isFinite(parallelism) || parallelism < 1) {
    errors.push("parallelism must be a positive number");
  }

  const maxAgents = Number(profile.maxAgents ?? parallelism);
  if (!Number.isFinite(maxAgents) || maxAgents < 1) {
    errors.push("maxAgents must be a positive number");
  }

  const ceiling = PARALLELISM_CEILING[String(profile.budgetClass)];
  if (parallelism > ceiling) {
    errors.push(`parallelism ${parallelism} exceeds budget ceiling ${ceiling} for ${profile.budgetClass}`);
  }

  if (maxAgents < parallelism) {
    errors.push("maxAgents must not be smaller than parallelism");
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(profile.stopRules) || profile.stopRules.length === 0) {
    errors.push("at least one stop rule is required");
  } else {
    for (const [index, rule] of profile.stopRules.entries()) {
      if (!isPlainObject(rule)) {
        errors.push(`stopRules[${index}] must be an object`);
        continue;
      }
      if (typeof rule.kind !== "string" || !rule.kind.trim()) {
        errors.push(`stopRules[${index}].kind is required`);
      }
      if (typeof rule.description !== "string" || !rule.description.trim()) {
        errors.push(`stopRules[${index}].description is required`);
      }
      if (stopRuleRequiresThreshold(String(rule.kind))) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(rule.threshold) || Number(rule.threshold) < 1) {
          errors.push(`stopRules[${index}] requires a positive threshold`);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (!isPlainObject(profile.outputContract)) {
    errors.push("outputContract is required");
  } else {
    if (typeof profile.outputContract.kind !== "string" || !profile.outputContract.kind.trim()) {
      errors.push("outputContract.kind is required");
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(profile.outputContract.artifactTypes) || profile.outputContract.artifactTypes.length === 0) {
      errors.push("outputContract.artifactTypes must be non-empty");
    }
  }

  if (!isPlainObject(profile.evidencePolicy) || typeof profile.evidencePolicy.mode !== "string") {
    errors.push("evidencePolicy.mode is required");
  }

  if (!isPlainObject(profile.mutationPolicy) || !MUTATION_MODES.includes(profile.mutationPolicy.mode as never)) {
    errors.push("mutationPolicy.mode is required");
  } else {
    if ((profile.mutationPolicy.mode === "preview_then_apply" || profile.mutationPolicy.mode === "apply_allowed") &&
      !isPlainObject(profile.riskPolicy)) {
      errors.push("riskPolicy is required for mutation-bearing profiles");
    }
  }

  if (!isPlainObject(profile.tracePolicy) || !TRACE_LEVELS.includes(profile.tracePolicy.level as never)) {
    errors.push("tracePolicy.level is required");
  }

  if (Array.isArray(profile.agentRoles) && profile.agentRoles.length > maxAgents) {
    warnings.push("agentRoles count exceeds maxAgents; some roles may never be scheduled");
  }

  if ((profile.evidencePolicy as Record<string, unknown>)?.mode === "required" &&
    typeof (profile.evidencePolicy as Record<string, unknown>)?.sourceBudget !== "number") {
    warnings.push("required evidence profile should declare sourceBudget");
  }

  return { valid: errors.length === 0, errors, warnings };
}

export function validateWorkOrderPreview(preview: unknown): RunProfileValidationResult {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const warnings: string[] = [];

  if (!isPlainObject(preview)) {
    return { valid: false, errors: ["preview must be an object"], warnings };
  }

  if (typeof preview.profileId !== "string" || !preview.profileId.trim()) errors.push("profileId is required");
  if (typeof preview.profileName !== "string" || !preview.profileName.trim()) errors.push("profileName is required");
  if (!Array.isArray(preview.agentRoles)) errors.push("agentRoles array is required");

  if (!isPlainObject(preview.estimate)) {
    errors.push("estimate is required");
  } else {
    if (typeof preview.estimate.estimatedTokens !== "number") errors.push("estimate.estimatedTokens is required");
    if (typeof preview.estimate.estimatedSteps !== "number") errors.push("estimate.estimatedSteps is required");
  }

  if (!Array.isArray(preview.stopRules) || preview.stopRules.length === 0) {
    errors.push("preview must retain stopRules");
  }

  if (!isPlainObject(preview.outputContract)) {
    errors.push("preview must retain outputContract");
  }

  if ("executed" in preview || "applied" in preview) {
    errors.push("preview must not claim execution or application state");
  }

  return { valid: errors.length === 0, errors, warnings };
}

import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import {
  CORE_PRESETS,
  buildWorkOrderPreview,
  estimateRunBudget,
  toRunProfileUISchema,
  validateRunProfile,
  validateWorkOrderPreview,
} from "../src/index.ts";

test("all core presets validate", () => {
  for (const preset of CORE_PRESETS) {
    const result = validateRunProfile(preset);
    assert.equal(result.valid, true, `${preset.id} should validate`);
  }
});

test("budget estimate is deterministic and bounded", () => {
  const estimate = estimateRunBudget(CORE_PRESETS[0]);
  assert.ok(estimate.estimatedTokens > 0);
  assert.ok(estimate.estimatedSteps >= 2);
  assert.equal(estimate.estimatedParallelLanes, 1);
});

test("ui schema mirrors core state", () => {
  const schema = toRunProfileUISchema(CORE_PRESETS[3]);
  assert.equal(schema.title, CORE_PRESETS[3].name);
  assert.match(schema.runButtonLabel, /Run|Review/);
  assert.ok(schema.outputBadge.label.length > 0);
});

test("work order preview validates", () => {
  const preview = buildWorkOrderPreview(CORE_PRESETS[1]);
  const result = validateWorkOrderPreview(preview);
  assert.equal(result.valid, true);
});

test("domain leakage into core is rejected", () => {
  const leaked = {
    ...CORE_PRESETS[0],
    targetSolutionsN: 3,
  };
  const result = validateRunProfile(leaked);
  assert.equal(result.valid, false);
  assert.ok(result.errors.some((error) => error.includes("targetSolutionsN")));
});

test("invalid profile without output contract is rejected", () => {
  const invalid = { ...CORE_PRESETS[0], outputContract: { kind: "", artifactTypes: [] } };
  const result = validateRunProfile(invalid);
  assert.equal(result.valid, false);
});

test("invalid stop rule is rejected", () => {
  const invalid = {
    ...CORE_PRESETS[0],
    stopRules: [
      {
        kind: "target_outputs_reached",
        description: "missing threshold should fail",
      },
    ],
  };
  const result = validateRunProfile(invalid);
  assert.equal(result.valid, false);
  assert.ok(result.errors.some((error) => error.includes("positive threshold")));
});

function Finalize-ActiveOrgan {
  param(
    [string]$OrganId,
    [bool]$HasDeficit,
    [bool]$HasEval,
    [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]$MissingDeficits,
    [System.Collections.Generic.List[string]]$MissingEvals
  )
  if ($null -ne $OrganId) {
    if (-not $HasDeficit) { $MissingDeficits.Add($OrganId) }
    if (-not $HasEval) { $MissingEvals.Add($OrganId) }
  }
}

foreach ($line in $lines) {
  if ($line -match '^[A-Za-z0-9_]+:\s*$') {
    if ($insideActiveOrgan -and $section -eq "active_organs") {
      Finalize-ActiveOrgan -OrganId $currentOrganId -HasDeficit $currentHasDeficit -HasEval $currentHasEval -MissingDeficits $activeOrganMissingDeficits -MissingEvals $activeOrganMissingEvals
      $insideActiveOrgan = $false
      $currentOrganId = $null
      $currentHasDeficit = $false
      $currentHasEval = $false
      $subsection = ""
    }
    $insideFirstBatch = ($line.Trim() -eq "first_real_install_batch:")
    $section = $line.Trim()
    continue
  }

  switch ($section) {
    "copy_files:" {
      if ($line -match '^\s*-\s+"?([^"]+)"?\s*$') { $copyFiles.Add($matches[1]) }
    }
    "reference_only_files:" {
      if ($line -match '^\s*-\s+"?([^"]+)"?\s*$') { $referenceFiles.Add($matches[1]) }
    }
    "forbidden_to_copy:" {
      if ($line -match '^\s*-\s+"?([^"]+)"?\s*$') { $forbiddenFiles.Add($matches[1]) }
    }
    "active_organs:" {
      if ($line -match '^\s*-\s+id:\s+"?([^"]+)"?\s*$') {
        if ($insideActiveOrgan) {
          Finalize-ActiveOrgan -OrganId $currentOrganId -HasDeficit $currentHasDeficit -HasEval $currentHasEval -MissingDeficits $activeOrganMissingDeficits -MissingEvals $activeOrganMissingEvals
        }
        $insideActiveOrgan = $true
        $currentOrganId = $matches[1]
        if ($activeOrganIds -contains $currentOrganId) {
          $duplicateOrganIds.Add($currentOrganId)
        } else {
          $activeOrganIds.Add($currentOrganId)
        }
        $currentHasDeficit = $false
        $currentHasEval = $false
        $subsection = ""
        continue
      }
      if ($insideActiveOrgan -and $line -match '^\s+linked_deficits:\s*$') {
        $subsection = "linked_deficits"
        continue
      }
      if ($insideActiveOrgan -and $line -match '^\s+linked_evals:\s*$') {
        $subsection = "linked_evals"
        continue
      }
      if ($insideActiveOrgan -and $subsection -eq "linked_deficits" -and $line -match '^\s*-\s+"?DEFICIT-[^"]+"?\s*$') {
        $currentHasDeficit = $true
        continue
      }
      if ($insideActiveOrgan -and $subsection -eq "linked_evals" -and $line -match '^\s*-\s+"?EVAL-[^"]+"?\s*$') {
        $currentHasEval = $true
        continue
      }
    }
    "first_real_install_batch:" {
      if ($line -match '^\s+allowed_copy_files_if_no_collision:\s*$') {
        $subsection = "first_batch_allowed"
        continue
      }
      if ($line -match '^\s+generated_target_local_files_first_batch:\s*$') {
        $subsection = ""
        continue
      }
      if ($line -match '^\s+explicitly_not_first_batch:\s*$') {
        $subsection = "first_batch_blocked"
        continue
      }
      if ($line -match '^\s+forbidden_actions:\s*$') {
        $subsection = ""
        continue
      }
      if ($subsection -eq "first_batch_allowed" -and $line -match '^\s*-\s+"?([^"]+)"?\s*$') {
        $firstBatchAllowedCopyFiles.Add($matches[1])
        continue
      }
      if ($subsection -eq "first_batch_blocked" -and $line -match '^\s*-\s+"?([^"]+)"?\s*$') {
        $firstBatchBlockedTargets.Add($matches[1])
        continue
      }
    }
  }
}

if ($insideActiveOrgan) {
  Finalize-ActiveOrgan -OrganId $currentOrganId -HasDeficit $currentHasDeficit -HasEval $currentHasEval -MissingDeficits $activeOrganMissingDeficits -MissingEvals $activeOrganMissingEvals
}

$copyVsForbidden = $copyFiles | Where-Object { $forbiddenFiles -contains $_ }
$copyVsReference = $copyFiles | Where-Object { $referenceFiles -contains $_ }
$forbiddenFirstBatchTargets = @(
  "litops/prompt_engine.py",
  "litops/web/app.py",
  "litops/contract_run.py",
  "litops/context_assembler.py",
  "litops/web/templates/segment_detail.html",
  ".claude/settings.json",
  ".claude/hooks/prompt_guard.py",
  ".claude/hooks/pretool_guard.py",
  ".claude/hooks/stop_guard.py"
)

$firstBatchIllegalAllowed = $firstBatchAllowedCopyFiles | Where-Object {
  $_ -in @(
    "04_KITS/v0_minimal/.claude/settings.json",
    "04_KITS/v0_minimal/.claude/hooks/prompt_guard.py",
    "04_KITS/v0_minimal/.claude/hooks/pretool_guard.py",
    "04_KITS/v0_minimal/.claude/hooks/stop_guard.py"
  )
}

$missingBlockedTargets = $forbiddenFirstBatchTargets | Where-Object {
  $firstBatchBlockedTargets -notcontains $_
}

if ($duplicateOrganIds.Count -gt 0) {
  Write-Error ("Duplicate active organ IDs: " + (($duplicateOrganIds | Select-Object -Unique) -join ", "))
  exit 1
}

if ($activeOrganMissingDeficits.Count -gt 0) {
  Write-Error ("Active organs missing deficit refs: " + ($activeOrganMissingDeficits -join ", "))
  exit 1
}

if ($activeOrganMissingEvals.Count -gt 0) {
  Write-Error ("Active organs missing eval refs: " + ($activeOrganMissingEvals -join ", "))
  exit 1
}

if ($copyVsForbidden.Count -gt 0) {
  Write-Error ("Files listed in both copy_files and forbidden_to_copy: " + (($copyVsForbidden | Select-Object -Unique) -join ", "))
  exit 1
}

if ($copyVsReference.Count -gt 0) {
  Write-Error ("Files listed in both copy_files and reference_only_files: " + (($copyVsReference | Select-Object -Unique) -join ", "))
  exit 1
}

if ($manifestText -notmatch "Target host working tree is dirty\." -and $manifestText -notmatch "dirty host tree") {
  Write-Error "Dirty-tree stop condition is not represented."
  exit 1
}

if ($firstBatchIllegalAllowed.Count -gt 0) {
  Write-Error ("First batch illegally allows hardened runtime files: " + (($firstBatchIllegalAllowed | Select-Object -Unique) -join ", "))
  exit 1
}

if ($missingBlockedTargets.Count -gt 0) {
  Write-Error ("First batch missing blocked hardened targets: " + ($missingBlockedTargets -join ", "))
  exit 1
}

Write-Output "manifest=ok"
Write-Output ("active_organs=" + $activeOrganIds.Count)
Write-Output ("copy_files=" + $copyFiles.Count)
Write-Output ("first_batch_allowed_copy_files=" + $firstBatchAllowedCopyFiles.Count)
Write-Output ("first_batch_blocked_targets=" + $firstBatchBlockedTargets.Count)
Write-Output ("reference_only_files=" + $referenceFiles.Count)
Write-Output ("forbidden_to_copy=" + $forbiddenFiles.Count)

# DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml
#
# Deficit registry for Agentum.
#
# This file stores recurrent project pressure that may require:
# - a new organ
# - a guard change
# - a profile/install correction
# - a memory or orchestration fix
#
# Schema:
# - id: stable deficit id
#   title: compact deficit name
#   status: open | testing | mitigated | resolved | archived
#   severity: low | medium | high | critical
#   observed_in_project:
#     - project or ecosystem scope
#   observed_in_layer:
#     - runtime | memory | prompt | retrieval | review | orchestration | install | governance
#   source_fragment:
#     - minimal source symptom or trigger
#   substrate_class:
#     - chat | log | eval | review | install | manuscript | corpus | recovery | mixed
#   trace_class:
#     - runtime_trace | memory_trace | review_trace | eval_trace | operator_trace | install_trace | provenance_trace | manuscript_trace | recovery_trace
#   evidence_refs:
#     - file paths, failure ids, eval ids, or notes
#   repeated_count: exact or approximate recurrence marker
#   replay_condition: named replay path if repeated_count is approximate
#   evidence_density: normal | cross_project | critical | weak
#   failure_type:
#     - mismatch | omission | false_confidence | drift | overload | bleed | collapse | authority_confusion
#   semantic_loss: what meaning, continuity, or function is being damaged
#   suspected_cause:
#     - current best explanations
#   scene_signature:
#     - recurring scene where the deficit shows up
#   memory_signature:
#     - related memory regime confusion
#   required_capability:
#     - what capability is missing
#   current_workaround:
#     - how humans or existing organs currently compensate
#   candidate_organs:
#     - related organ ids or compact names
#   candidate_non_organ_fix:
#     - config / profile / guard / process alternatives
#   test_scene:
#     - bounded scene for validating resolution
#   evaluation_criteria:
#     - what counts as actual mitigation
#   resolved_by:
#     - selected organ, profile, guard, or process change
#   residual_risk:
#     - what remains dangerous even after mitigation
#   notes: optional notes

# FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml
#
# Failure mode registry for Agentum.
#
# This file stores the concrete failures the kit must prevent, detect, or route.
# It is the bridge between research and eval scenarios.
#
# Schema:
# - id: stable failure id
#   title: short failure name
#   symptom: observable symptom
#   trigger: common prompt/tool/context trigger
#   risk: why it is dangerous
#   guards: patterns / files / hooks / commands that should catch it
#   eval_scenarios: related eval scenario ids or names
#   sources: source ids from SOURCE_REGISTRY.yaml
#   patterns: pattern ids from PATTERN_REGISTRY.yaml
#   status: open | covered_v0 | covered_v1 | needs_test | research_only
#   notes: optional notes

failures:

# ORGAN_GOVERNANCE_LOOP

## Purpose

This document defines the minimal operating loop for keeping organ records, deficit records, eval bindings, and install scopes aligned.

The goal is not committee theater.

The goal is to keep the ecosystem from drifting back into:

- undocumented pressure;
- orphan organs;
- eval-free promotion;
- permanent half-life candidates;
- profile/install drift.

## Core loop

The minimal governance loop is:

1. observe pressure
2. write or update `Deficit Record`
3. decide whether a non-organ fix is enough
4. if not, generate bounded candidate set
5. run scene test, critic pass, and semantic-capital gate
6. promote / reject / quarantine
7. update organ registry
8. update eval matrix
9. update affected profiles and install scopes

## Trigger conditions

Run the loop when any of these happens:

- a deficit repeats;
- an organ is proposed;
- an organ boundary changes;
- an eval gap becomes visible;
- a profile needs a new organ set;
- a target install exposes a missing guard or false organ assumption.

## Required artifacts per pass

Each pass should leave behind, at minimum:

- one updated `Deficit Record` or explicit no-deficit result
- one candidate note or explicit non-organ decision
- one critic result
- one semantic-capital decision if organ birth is considered
- one registry or matrix update if promotion occurs

No invisible governance.

## Decision outputs

The loop may end in only a small number of lawful states:

- `reject`
- `revise`
- `quarantine`
- `select`
- `profile_only_fix`
- `guard_only_fix`
- `demote_to_fragment_or_policy`
- `deprecate_or_retire_existing_organ`

This keeps the ecosystem from pretending every pressure needs a new organ.

## Cross-layer updates

If an organ is selected, check these surfaces:

- `02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml`
- `02_REGISTRIES/DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml`
- `06_EVALS/ORGAN_DEFICIT_EVAL_MATRIX.md`
- affected `05_PROFILES/*`
- affected `08_INSTALL/*`

If these are not considered, governance is incomplete.

## Anti-patterns

Watch for:

- theory update with no registry consequence;
- registry update with no eval consequence;
- install change with no named active organ set;
- profile claiming an organ without its deficit or boundary;
- organ proliferation with no retirement path.

## Practical rule

The governance loop should stay boringly explicit.

If the ecosystem cannot say:

- what pressure appeared,
- what answered it,
- how it was tested,
- where it may be installed,

then governance has failed, even if the prose sounds grand.

# ORGAN_DEFICIT_EVAL_MATRIX

## Purpose

This matrix binds three surfaces that should no longer drift apart:

- recurrent deficits
- candidate or stable organs
- eval scenarios

The goal is simple:

if an organ claims to answer a deficit, the ecosystem must know which evals test that claim.

## How to read this matrix

Each row answers:

- what deficit exists;
- which organ currently answers it;
- which eval scenarios test the answer;
- what remains unresolved.

This is a governance map, not a runtime prompt.

## Matrix

| Deficit | Pressure summary | Primary organs | Supporting organs | Eval scenarios | Current status | Remaining gap |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `DEFICIT-001` | foreign prompt or wrong-project execution displaces active identity | `ORGAN-001`, `ORGAN-006` | `ORGAN-005` | `EVAL-01`, `EVAL-10`, `EVAL-15`, `EVAL-23` | mitigated | cross-project ecosystems still need stronger install-bound identity checks |
| `DEFICIT-002` | meaning fragments drop, distort, or vanish in active assembly | `ORGAN-003` | `ORGAN-002`, `ORGAN-006` | `EVAL-07`, `EVAL-15`, `EVAL-16`, `EVAL-24` | open | dedicated eval now exists, but it still needs repeated practical use and addressee-aware field reporting |
| `DEFICIT-003` | recovered or old material becomes false authority | `ORGAN-003`, `ORGAN-006` | `ORGAN-005` | `EVAL-06`, `EVAL-07`, `EVAL-15`, `EVAL-21` | mitigated | recovery still needs more explicit epistemic-status testing |
| `DEFICIT-004` | pseudo-rigor and false necessity pass without real critic pressure | `ORGAN-005` | `ORGAN-006` | `EVAL-09`, `EVAL-12`, `EVAL-14`, `EVAL-17`, `EVAL-22`, `EVAL-24` | open | critic-pass eval now exists, but it still needs repeated practical use |
| `DEFICIT-005` | repeated pressure does not become lawful organ birth | `ORGAN-006` | `ORGAN-005` | `EVAL-14`, `EVAL-15`, `EVAL-17`, `EVAL-18`, `EVAL-21`, `EVAL-22`, `EVAL-23` | open | Foundry evals now exist, but the pipeline is still theory-first rather than run-proven |

## Organ-centered view

### `ORGAN-001` Ingress Membrane

- protects against:
  - foreign ingress without identity check
  - raw trace loss
  - early prompt obedience
- tested mainly by:
  - `EVAL-01`
  - `EVAL-10`
  - `EVAL-15`
  - `EVAL-23`

### `ORGAN-002` Litops Digestion Organ

- protects against:
  - raw corpus overload
  - provenance loss during transformation
  - undifferentiated source mass
- tested indirectly by:
  - `EVAL-06`
  - `EVAL-11`
  - `EVAL-20`
  - `EVAL-24`
- gap:
  - digestion eval now exists, but context-pack field use is not yet repeatedly exercised

### `ORGAN-003` MemoryWorkbench Reconstructive Memory

- protects against:
  - continuity collapse
  - memory as raw retrieval only
  - false authority from recall
- tested mainly by:
  - `EVAL-06`
  - `EVAL-07`
  - `EVAL-12`
  - `EVAL-15`
  - `EVAL-16`
  - `EVAL-21`
  - `EVAL-24`
- gap:
  - new fragment-fate eval exists but still needs repeated practical use

### `ORGAN-004` WhiteCrow Manuscript Field Organ

- protects against:
  - ungrounded rewrite
  - source drift
  - manuscript field collapse
- tested mainly by:
  - `EVAL-11`
  - `EVAL-19`
- gap:
  - cite-back eval now exists, but real manuscript patch loops are not yet repeatedly exercised

### `ORGAN-005` Mindlock Critic Organ

- protects against:
  - pseudo-rigor
  - fake completion
  - false necessity
  - ornamental method
- tested mainly by:
  - `EVAL-09`
  - `EVAL-12`
  - `EVAL-14`
  - `EVAL-17`
  - `EVAL-22`
- gap:
  - critic-pass eval exists, but selection loops are not yet field-proven

### `ORGAN-006` Agentum Governance and Immune Organ

- protects against:
  - project bleed
  - guard drift
  - false readiness
  - cross-project governance decay
- tested mainly by:
  - `EVAL-01`
  - `EVAL-10`
  - `EVAL-14`
  - `EVAL-15`
  - `EVAL-21`
  - `EVAL-22`
  - `EVAL-23`
- gap:
  - profile-install eval coverage is still partial

## Newly added evals

The matrix has now been extended with these explicit new eval routes:

1. `context observability` eval
2. `candidate organ critic pass` eval
3. `Foundry birth` eval
4. `manuscript cite-back field` eval
5. `digestion provenance integrity` eval
6. `trace-grounded deficit` eval
7. `semantic capital gate` eval
8. `activation ceiling` eval
9. `profile vs addressee split` eval

These close the earlier conceptual gap.

The remaining gap is no longer "missing eval definitions."

It is lack of repeated field use and evidence from real install and evolution cycles.

## Minimal next binding rule

From this point on:

- no new deficit should be added without at least one proposed eval route;
- no new stable organ should be added without eval references;
- no selected organ should remain unbound from the matrix for long.

Otherwise the Foundry becomes decorative and the registries drift back into theory-only status.

+# Registry contracts
+
+This directory contains executable, machine-readable contracts for Agentum registry surfaces. It preserves the source registries as YAML and does not turn theory or the research archive into runtime payload.
+
+`agent_organ_record.schema.json` encodes the record shape from `03_THEORY/AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC.md`; `scripts/agent_organ_registry.py` adds the checks JSON Schema cannot express cleanly: known relation IDs, duplicate IDs, lifecycle/trust compatibility, and self-lineage prevention.
+
+The current contract deliberately validates only the compact registry lifecycle (`draft`, `candidate`, `selected`, `active`, `quarantined`, `retired`). The richer lifecycle remains governed by `03_THEORY/AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md` until the living registry adopts it.

diff --git a/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py b/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c05d06d83cfa61f61010a9ee46c6fbc27c016a63

@@ -0,0 +1,156 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate Agentum AgentOrgan records and emit deterministic registry hygiene data."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import yaml
+from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_SCHEMA = ROOT / "registry_contracts" / "agent_organ_record.schema.json"
+DEFAULT_DEFICITS = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_FAILURES = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_REPORT = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "derived" / "agent_organ_registry_report.json"
+CONTRACT_VERSION = "1.0.0"
+ID_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:ORGAN|DEFICIT|FAILURE)-[0-9]{3,}$")
+TRUST_RANK = {"theory_only": 0, "scene_tested": 1, "install_tested": 2, "field_validated": 3}
+
+
+def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        value = yaml.safe_load(source)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a YAML mapping at document root")
+    return value
+
+
+def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        return json.load(source)
+
+
+def known_ids(path: Path, collection: str) -> set[str]:
+    records = load_yaml(path).get(collection, [])
+    return {record["id"] for record in records if isinstance(record, dict) and isinstance(record.get("id"), str)}
+
+
+def validate(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], deficit_ids: set[str], failure_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    for issue in sorted(Draft202012Validator(schema).iter_errors(data), key=lambda item: list(item.absolute_path)):
+        location = "/".join(str(item) for item in issue.absolute_path) or "root"
+        errors.append(f"schema {location}: {issue.message}")
+
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    if not isinstance(organs, list):
+        return errors, warnings
+    organ_ids = [record.get("id") for record in organs if isinstance(record, dict)]
+    duplicates = sorted({item for item, count in Counter(organ_ids).items() if isinstance(item, str) and count > 1})
+    for organ_id in duplicates:
+        errors.append(f"duplicate organ id: {organ_id}")
+
+    known_organs = {item for item in organ_ids if isinstance(item, str)}
+    for index, organ in enumerate(organs):
+        if not isinstance(organ, dict):
+            continue
+        label = organ.get("id", f"organs[{index}]")
+        for field, candidates, known, noun in (
+            ("deficit_origin", organ.get("deficit_origin", []), deficit_ids, "deficit"),
+            ("failure_modes", organ.get("failure_modes", []), failure_ids, "failure mode"),
+            ("parent_organs", organ.get("parent_organs", []), known_organs, "parent organ"),
+            ("child_organs", organ.get("child_organs", []), known_organs, "child organ"),
+        ):
+            if not isinstance(candidates, list):
+                continue
+            for candidate in candidates:
+                if isinstance(candidate, str) and ID_RE.match(candidate) and candidate not in known:
+                    errors.append(f"{label}: {field} references unknown {noun} {candidate}")
+        status, trust = organ.get("status"), organ.get("trust_level")
+        if status in {"selected", "active"} and trust not in {"scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: {status} requires at least scene_tested trust")
+        if status == "active" and trust not in {"install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: active requires install_tested or field_validated trust")
+        if status == "quarantined" and organ.get("quarantine_status") == "none":
+            errors.append(f"{label}: quarantined status requires bounded or strict quarantine_status")
+        if trust == "legacy_only" and status not in {"retired", "quarantined"}:
+            warnings.append(f"{label}: legacy_only trust is normally retired or quarantined")
+        parents, children = organ.get("parent_organs", []), organ.get("child_organs", [])
+        if isinstance(parents, list) and label in parents:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own parent")
+        if isinstance(children, list) and label in children:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own child")
+    return errors, warnings
+
+
+def build_report(data: dict[str, Any], registry_path: Path, errors: list[str], warnings: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    inventory = [
+        {"id": organ["id"], "name": organ["name"], "status": organ["status"], "trust_level": organ["trust_level"], "quarantine_status": organ["quarantine_status"], "output_class": organ["output_class"]}
+        for organ in organs if isinstance(organ, dict) and all(key in organ for key in ("id", "name", "status", "trust_level", "quarantine_status", "output_class"))
+    ]
+    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(registry_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "contract": {"name": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record", "version": CONTRACT_VERSION, "schema": "registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json"},
+        "source": {"path": "02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml", "sha256": source_hash},
+        "valid": not errors,
+        "errors": errors,
+        "warnings": warnings,
+        "summary": {
+            "organ_count": len(inventory),
+            "by_status": dict(sorted(Counter(item["status"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_trust_level": dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_status_and_trust": {
+                status: dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory if item["status"] == status).items()))
+                for status in sorted({item["status"] for item in inventory})
+            },
+        },
+        "inventory": sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: item["id"]),
+    }
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    for command in ("validate", "report"):
+        subparser = subcommands.add_parser(command)
+        subparser.add_argument("--registry", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        subparser.add_argument("--schema", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        subparser.add_argument("--deficits", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DEFICITS)
+        subparser.add_argument("--failures", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_FAILURES)
+        if command == "report":
+            subparser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REPORT)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        data = load_yaml(args.registry)
+        errors, warnings = validate(data, load_schema(args.schema), known_ids(args.deficits, "deficits"), known_ids(args.failures, "failures"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError, yaml.YAMLError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exception:
+        print(f"agent-organ-registry: {exception}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    if args.command == "report":
+        report = build_report(data, args.registry, errors, warnings)
+        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"report={args.output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
+    else:
+        print(f"organs={len(data.get('organs', []))}")
+    for warning in warnings:
+        print(f"warning: {warning}")
+    for error in errors:
+        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
+    print("agent_organ_registry=ok" if not errors else "agent_organ_registry=invalid")
+    return 0 if not errors else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())
diff --git a/tests/test_agent_organ_registry.py b/tests/test_agent_organ_registry.py

@@ -0,0 +1,51 @@
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_organ_registry", ROOT / "scripts" / "agent_organ_registry.py")
+assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
+registry_tool = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
+SPEC.loader.exec_module(registry_tool)
+
+
+class AgentOrganRegistryTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
+        cls.schema = registry_tool.load_schema(registry_tool.DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        cls.data = registry_tool.load_yaml(registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        cls.deficits = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_DEFICITS, "deficits")
+        cls.failures = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_FAILURES, "failures")
+
+    def test_actual_registry_validates(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertEqual(errors, [])
+        self.assertEqual(warnings, [])
+
+    def test_unknown_references_are_rejected(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], parent_organs=["ORGAN-999"])]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("unknown parent organ ORGAN-999" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_active_requires_install_trust(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], status="active", trust_level="scene_tested")]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("active requires install_tested" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_report_is_deterministic_and_consumable(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        report = registry_tool.build_report(self.data, registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY, errors, warnings)
+        self.assertTrue(report["valid"])
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["organ_count"], 7)
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_status"], {"active": 1, "candidate": 1, "selected": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_trust_level"], {"install_tested": 2, "scene_tested": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(json.dumps(report))["inventory"][0]["id"], "ORGAN-001")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# Derived registry outputs
+
+These files are reproducible hygiene artifacts, not source-of-truth registry records.
+
+Regenerate `agent_organ_registry_report.json` from the repository root:
+
+```powershell
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py validate
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py report
+```
+
+The report is deterministic for a given source registry: it includes the source SHA-256 and does not include a generation timestamp.

+# Registry contracts
+
+This directory contains executable, machine-readable contracts for Agentum registry surfaces. It preserves the source registries as YAML and does not turn theory or the research archive into runtime payload.
+
+`agent_organ_record.schema.json` encodes the record shape from `03_THEORY/AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC.md`; `scripts/agent_organ_registry.py` adds the checks JSON Schema cannot express cleanly: known relation IDs, duplicate IDs, lifecycle/trust compatibility, and self-lineage prevention.
+
+The current contract deliberately validates only the compact registry lifecycle (`draft`, `candidate`, `selected`, `active`, `quarantined`, `retired`). The richer lifecycle remains governed by `03_THEORY/AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md` until the living registry adopts it.

diff --git a/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py b/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c05d06d83cfa61f61010a9ee46c6fbc27c016a63

+++ b/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py
@@ -0,0 +1,156 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate Agentum AgentOrgan records and emit deterministic registry hygiene data."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import yaml
+from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_SCHEMA = ROOT / "registry_contracts" / "agent_organ_record.schema.json"
+DEFAULT_DEFICITS = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_FAILURES = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_REPORT = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "derived" / "agent_organ_registry_report.json"
+CONTRACT_VERSION = "1.0.0"
+ID_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:ORGAN|DEFICIT|FAILURE)-[0-9]{3,}$")
+TRUST_RANK = {"theory_only": 0, "scene_tested": 1, "install_tested": 2, "field_validated": 3}
+
+
+def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        value = yaml.safe_load(source)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a YAML mapping at document root")
+    return value
+
+
+def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        return json.load(source)
+
+
+def known_ids(path: Path, collection: str) -> set[str]:
+    records = load_yaml(path).get(collection, [])
+    return {record["id"] for record in records if isinstance(record, dict) and isinstance(record.get("id"), str)}
+
+
+def validate(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], deficit_ids: set[str], failure_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    for issue in sorted(Draft202012Validator(schema).iter_errors(data), key=lambda item: list(item.absolute_path)):
+        location = "/".join(str(item) for item in issue.absolute_path) or "root"
+        errors.append(f"schema {location}: {issue.message}")
+
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    if not isinstance(organs, list):
+        return errors, warnings
+    organ_ids = [record.get("id") for record in organs if isinstance(record, dict)]
+    duplicates = sorted({item for item, count in Counter(organ_ids).items() if isinstance(item, str) and count > 1})
+    for organ_id in duplicates:
+        errors.append(f"duplicate organ id: {organ_id}")
+
+    known_organs = {item for item in organ_ids if isinstance(item, str)}
+    for index, organ in enumerate(organs):
+        if not isinstance(organ, dict):
+            continue
+        label = organ.get("id", f"organs[{index}]")
+        for field, candidates, known, noun in (
+            ("deficit_origin", organ.get("deficit_origin", []), deficit_ids, "deficit"),
+            ("failure_modes", organ.get("failure_modes", []), failure_ids, "failure mode"),
+            ("parent_organs", organ.get("parent_organs", []), known_organs, "parent organ"),
+            ("child_organs", organ.get("child_organs", []), known_organs, "child organ"),
+        ):
+            if not isinstance(candidates, list):
+                continue
+            for candidate in candidates:
+                if isinstance(candidate, str) and ID_RE.match(candidate) and candidate not in known:
+                    errors.append(f"{label}: {field} references unknown {noun} {candidate}")
+        status, trust = organ.get("status"), organ.get("trust_level")
+        if status in {"selected", "active"} and trust not in {"scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: {status} requires at least scene_tested trust")
+        if status == "active" and trust not in {"install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: active requires install_tested or field_validated trust")
+        if status == "quarantined" and organ.get("quarantine_status") == "none":
+            errors.append(f"{label}: quarantined status requires bounded or strict quarantine_status")
+        if trust == "legacy_only" and status not in {"retired", "quarantined"}:
+            warnings.append(f"{label}: legacy_only trust is normally retired or quarantined")
+        parents, children = organ.get("parent_organs", []), organ.get("child_organs", [])
+        if isinstance(parents, list) and label in parents:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own parent")
+        if isinstance(children, list) and label in children:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own child")
+    return errors, warnings
+
+
+def build_report(data: dict[str, Any], registry_path: Path, errors: list[str], warnings: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    inventory = [
+        {"id": organ["id"], "name": organ["name"], "status": organ["status"], "trust_level": organ["trust_level"], "quarantine_status": organ["quarantine_status"], "output_class": organ["output_class"]}
+        for organ in organs if isinstance(organ, dict) and all(key in organ for key in ("id", "name", "status", "trust_level", "quarantine_status", "output_class"))
+    ]
+    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(registry_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "contract": {"name": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record", "version": CONTRACT_VERSION, "schema": "registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json"},
+        "source": {"path": "02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml", "sha256": source_hash},
+        "valid": not errors,
+        "errors": errors,
+        "warnings": warnings,
+        "summary": {
+            "organ_count": len(inventory),
+            "by_status": dict(sorted(Counter(item["status"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_trust_level": dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_status_and_trust": {
+                status: dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory if item["status"] == status).items()))
+                for status in sorted({item["status"] for item in inventory})
+            },
+        },
+        "inventory": sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: item["id"]),
+    }
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    for command in ("validate", "report"):
+        subparser = subcommands.add_parser(command)
+        subparser.add_argument("--registry", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        subparser.add_argument("--schema", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        subparser.add_argument("--deficits", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DEFICITS)
+        subparser.add_argument("--failures", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_FAILURES)
+        if command == "report":
+            subparser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REPORT)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        data = load_yaml(args.registry)
+        errors, warnings = validate(data, load_schema(args.schema), known_ids(args.deficits, "deficits"), known_ids(args.failures, "failures"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError, yaml.YAMLError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exception:
+        print(f"agent-organ-registry: {exception}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    if args.command == "report":
+        report = build_report(data, args.registry, errors, warnings)
+        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"report={args.output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
+    else:
+        print(f"organs={len(data.get('organs', []))}")
+    for warning in warnings:
+        print(f"warning: {warning}")
+    for error in errors:
+        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
+    print("agent_organ_registry=ok" if not errors else "agent_organ_registry=invalid")
+    return 0 if not errors else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+    },

diff --git a/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py b/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c05d06d83cfa61f61010a9ee46c6fbc27c016a63

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate Agentum AgentOrgan records and emit deterministic registry hygiene data."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import yaml
+from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_SCHEMA = ROOT / "registry_contracts" / "agent_organ_record.schema.json"
+DEFAULT_DEFICITS = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_FAILURES = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_REPORT = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "derived" / "agent_organ_registry_report.json"
+CONTRACT_VERSION = "1.0.0"
+ID_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:ORGAN|DEFICIT|FAILURE)-[0-9]{3,}$")
+TRUST_RANK = {"theory_only": 0, "scene_tested": 1, "install_tested": 2, "field_validated": 3}
+
+
+def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        value = yaml.safe_load(source)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a YAML mapping at document root")
+    return value
+
+
+def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        return json.load(source)
+
+
+def known_ids(path: Path, collection: str) -> set[str]:
+    records = load_yaml(path).get(collection, [])
+    return {record["id"] for record in records if isinstance(record, dict) and isinstance(record.get("id"), str)}
+
+
+def validate(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], deficit_ids: set[str], failure_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    for issue in sorted(Draft202012Validator(schema).iter_errors(data), key=lambda item: list(item.absolute_path)):
+        location = "/".join(str(item) for item in issue.absolute_path) or "root"
+        errors.append(f"schema {location}: {issue.message}")
+
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    if not isinstance(organs, list):
+        return errors, warnings
+    organ_ids = [record.get("id") for record in organs if isinstance(record, dict)]
+    duplicates = sorted({item for item, count in Counter(organ_ids).items() if isinstance(item, str) and count > 1})
+    for organ_id in duplicates:
+        errors.append(f"duplicate organ id: {organ_id}")
+
+    known_organs = {item for item in organ_ids if isinstance(item, str)}
+    for index, organ in enumerate(organs):
+        if not isinstance(organ, dict):
+            continue
+        label = organ.get("id", f"organs[{index}]")
+        for field, candidates, known, noun in (
+            ("deficit_origin", organ.get("deficit_origin", []), deficit_ids, "deficit"),
+            ("failure_modes", organ.get("failure_modes", []), failure_ids, "failure mode"),
+            ("parent_organs", organ.get("parent_organs", []), known_organs, "parent organ"),
+            ("child_organs", organ.get("child_organs", []), known_organs, "child organ"),
+        ):
+            if not isinstance(candidates, list):
+                continue
+            for candidate in candidates:
+                if isinstance(candidate, str) and ID_RE.match(candidate) and candidate not in known:
+                    errors.append(f"{label}: {field} references unknown {noun} {candidate}")
+        status, trust = organ.get("status"), organ.get("trust_level")
+        if status in {"selected", "active"} and trust not in {"scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: {status} requires at least scene_tested trust")
+        if status == "active" and trust not in {"install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: active requires install_tested or field_validated trust")
+        if status == "quarantined" and organ.get("quarantine_status") == "none":
+            errors.append(f"{label}: quarantined status requires bounded or strict quarantine_status")
+        if trust == "legacy_only" and status not in {"retired", "quarantined"}:
+            warnings.append(f"{label}: legacy_only trust is normally retired or quarantined")
+        parents, children = organ.get("parent_organs", []), organ.get("child_organs", [])
+        if isinstance(parents, list) and label in parents:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own parent")
+        if isinstance(children, list) and label in children:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own child")
+    return errors, warnings
+
+
+def build_report(data: dict[str, Any], registry_path: Path, errors: list[str], warnings: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    inventory = [
+        {"id": organ["id"], "name": organ["name"], "status": organ["status"], "trust_level": organ["trust_level"], "quarantine_status": organ["quarantine_status"], "output_class": organ["output_class"]}
+        for organ in organs if isinstance(organ, dict) and all(key in organ for key in ("id", "name", "status", "trust_level", "quarantine_status", "output_class"))
+    ]
+    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(registry_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "contract": {"name": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record", "version": CONTRACT_VERSION, "schema": "registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json"},
+        "source": {"path": "02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml", "sha256": source_hash},
+        "valid": not errors,
+        "errors": errors,
+        "warnings": warnings,
+        "summary": {
+            "organ_count": len(inventory),
+            "by_status": dict(sorted(Counter(item["status"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_trust_level": dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_status_and_trust": {
+                status: dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory if item["status"] == status).items()))
+                for status in sorted({item["status"] for item in inventory})
+            },
+        },
+        "inventory": sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: item["id"]),
+    }
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    for command in ("validate", "report"):
+        subparser = subcommands.add_parser(command)
+        subparser.add_argument("--registry", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        subparser.add_argument("--schema", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        subparser.add_argument("--deficits", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DEFICITS)
+        subparser.add_argument("--failures", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_FAILURES)
+        if command == "report":
+            subparser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REPORT)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        data = load_yaml(args.registry)
+        errors, warnings = validate(data, load_schema(args.schema), known_ids(args.deficits, "deficits"), known_ids(args.failures, "failures"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError, yaml.YAMLError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exception:
+        print(f"agent-organ-registry: {exception}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    if args.command == "report":
+        report = build_report(data, args.registry, errors, warnings)
+        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"report={args.output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
+    else:
+        print(f"organs={len(data.get('organs', []))}")
+    for warning in warnings:
+        print(f"warning: {warning}")
+    for error in errors:
+        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
+    print("agent_organ_registry=ok" if not errors else "agent_organ_registry=invalid")
+    return 0 if not errors else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())
