+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_organ_registry", ROOT / "scripts" / "agent_organ_registry.py")
+assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
+registry_tool = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
+SPEC.loader.exec_module(registry_tool)
+
+
+class AgentOrganRegistryTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
+        cls.schema = registry_tool.load_schema(registry_tool.DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        cls.data = registry_tool.load_yaml(registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        cls.deficits = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_DEFICITS, "deficits")
+        cls.failures = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_FAILURES, "failures")
+
+    def test_actual_registry_validates(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertEqual(errors, [])
+        self.assertEqual(warnings, [])
+
+    def test_unknown_references_are_rejected(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], parent_organs=["ORGAN-999"])]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("unknown parent organ ORGAN-999" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_active_requires_install_trust(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], status="active", trust_level="scene_tested")]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("active requires install_tested" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_report_is_deterministic_and_consumable(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        report = registry_tool.build_report(self.data, registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY, errors, warnings)
+        self.assertTrue(report["valid"])
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["organ_count"], 7)
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_status"], {"active": 1, "candidate": 1, "selected": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_trust_level"], {"install_tested": 2, "scene_tested": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(json.dumps(report))["inventory"][0]["id"], "ORGAN-001")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# Derived registry outputs
+
+These files are reproducible hygiene artifacts, not source-of-truth registry records.
+
+Regenerate `agent_organ_registry_report.json` from the repository root:
+
+```powershell
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py validate
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py report
+```
+
+The report is deterministic for a given source registry: it includes the source SHA-256 and does not include a generation timestamp.

+# Registry contracts
+
+This directory contains executable, machine-readable contracts for Agentum registry surfaces. It preserves the source registries as YAML and does not turn theory or the research archive into runtime payload.
+
+`agent_organ_record.schema.json` encodes the record shape from `03_THEORY/AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC.md`; `scripts/agent_organ_registry.py` adds the checks JSON Schema cannot express cleanly: known relation IDs, duplicate IDs, lifecycle/trust compatibility, and self-lineage prevention.
+
+The current contract deliberately validates only the compact registry lifecycle (`draft`, `candidate`, `selected`, `active`, `quarantined`, `retired`). The richer lifecycle remains governed by `03_THEORY/AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md` until the living registry adopts it.

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate Agentum AgentOrgan records and emit deterministic registry hygiene data."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import yaml
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_SCHEMA = ROOT / "registry_contracts" / "agent_organ_record.schema.json"
+DEFAULT_DEFICITS = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_FAILURES = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_REPORT = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "derived" / "agent_organ_registry_report.json"
+CONTRACT_VERSION = "1.0.0"
+ID_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:ORGAN|DEFICIT|FAILURE)-[0-9]{3,}$")
+TRUST_RANK = {"theory_only": 0, "scene_tested": 1, "install_tested": 2, "field_validated": 3}
+
+
+def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        value = yaml.safe_load(source)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a YAML mapping at document root")
+    return value
+
+
+def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        return json.load(source)
+
+
+def known_ids(path: Path, collection: str) -> set[str]:
+    records = load_yaml(path).get(collection, [])
+    return {record["id"] for record in records if isinstance(record, dict) and isinstance(record.get("id"), str)}
+
+
+def schema_errors(value: Any, rule: dict[str, Any], definitions: dict[str, Any], path: str = "root") -> list[str]:
+    """Validate the JSON-Schema subset used by this checked-in contract.
+
+    Keeping this small interpreter here avoids a second, undeclared validator runtime
+    while making the schema—not a parallel Python field list—the shape authority.
+    """
+    if "$ref" in rule:
+        reference = rule["$ref"]
+        if not reference.startswith("#/$defs/"):
+            return [f"schema {path}: unsupported reference {reference}"]
+        return schema_errors(value, definitions[reference.rsplit("/", 1)[1]], definitions, path)
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    if "enum" in rule and value not in rule["enum"]:
+        errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} is not one of {rule['enum']}")
+    expected = rule.get("type")
+    type_matches = {
+        "object": isinstance(value, dict),
+        "array": isinstance(value, list),
+        "string": isinstance(value, str),
+    }
+    if expected and not type_matches.get(expected, False):
+        return errors + [f"schema {path}: expected {expected}"]
+    if isinstance(value, str):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minLength", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: string is shorter than {rule['minLength']}")
+        if "pattern" in rule and not re.match(rule["pattern"], value):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} does not match {rule['pattern']}")
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minItems", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: requires at least {rule['minItems']} item(s)")
+        if "items" in rule:
+            for index, item in enumerate(value):
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(item, rule["items"], definitions, f"{path}/{index}"))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        properties = rule.get("properties", {})
+        for required in rule.get("required", []):
+            if required not in value:
+                errors.append(f"schema {path}: missing required property {required}")
+        if rule.get("additionalProperties") is False:
+            for key in value:
+                if key not in properties:
+                    errors.append(f"schema {path}: unknown property {key}")
+        for key, child_rule in properties.items():
+            if key in value:
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(value[key], child_rule, definitions, f"{path}/{key}"))
+    return errors
+
+
+def validate(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], deficit_ids: set[str], failure_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    errors.extend(schema_errors(data, schema, schema.get("$defs", {})))
+
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    if not isinstance(organs, list):
+        return errors, warnings
+    organ_ids = [record.get("id") for record in organs if isinstance(record, dict)]
+    duplicates = sorted({item for item, count in Counter(organ_ids).items() if isinstance(item, str) and count > 1})
+    for organ_id in duplicates:
+        errors.append(f"duplicate organ id: {organ_id}")
+
+    known_organs = {item for item in organ_ids if isinstance(item, str)}
+    for index, organ in enumerate(organs):
+        if not isinstance(organ, dict):
+            continue
+        label = organ.get("id", f"organs[{index}]")
+        for field, candidates, known, noun in (
+            ("deficit_origin", organ.get("deficit_origin", []), deficit_ids, "deficit"),
+            ("failure_modes", organ.get("failure_modes", []), failure_ids, "failure mode"),
+            ("parent_organs", organ.get("parent_organs", []), known_organs, "parent organ"),
+            ("child_organs", organ.get("child_organs", []), known_organs, "child organ"),
+        ):
+            if not isinstance(candidates, list):
+                continue
+            for candidate in candidates:
+                if isinstance(candidate, str) and ID_RE.match(candidate) and candidate not in known:
+                    errors.append(f"{label}: {field} references unknown {noun} {candidate}")
+        status, trust = organ.get("status"), organ.get("trust_level")
+        if status in {"selected", "active"} and trust not in {"scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: {status} requires at least scene_tested trust")
+        if status == "active" and trust not in {"install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: active requires install_tested or field_validated trust")
+        if status == "quarantined" and organ.get("quarantine_status") == "none":
+            errors.append(f"{label}: quarantined status requires bounded or strict quarantine_status")
+        if trust == "legacy_only" and status not in {"retired", "quarantined"}:
+            warnings.append(f"{label}: legacy_only trust is normally retired or quarantined")
+        parents, children = organ.get("parent_organs", []), organ.get("child_organs", [])
+        if isinstance(parents, list) and label in parents:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own parent")
+        if isinstance(children, list) and label in children:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own child")
+    return errors, warnings
+
+
+def build_report(data: dict[str, Any], registry_path: Path, errors: list[str], warnings: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    inventory = [
+        {"id": organ["id"], "name": organ["name"], "status": organ["status"], "trust_level": organ["trust_level"], "quarantine_status": organ["quarantine_status"], "output_class": organ["output_class"]}
+        for organ in organs if isinstance(organ, dict) and all(key in organ for key in ("id", "name", "status", "trust_level", "quarantine_status", "output_class"))
+    ]
+    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(registry_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "contract": {"name": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record", "version": CONTRACT_VERSION, "schema": "registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json"},
+        "source": {"path": "02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml", "sha256": source_hash},
+        "valid": not errors,
+        "errors": errors,
+        "warnings": warnings,
+        "summary": {
+            "organ_count": len(inventory),
+            "by_status": dict(sorted(Counter(item["status"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_trust_level": dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_status_and_trust": {
+                status: dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory if item["status"] == status).items()))
+                for status in sorted({item["status"] for item in inventory})
+            },
+        },
+        "inventory": sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: item["id"]),
+    }
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    for command in ("validate", "report"):
+        subparser = subcommands.add_parser(command)
+        subparser.add_argument("--registry", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        subparser.add_argument("--schema", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        subparser.add_argument("--deficits", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DEFICITS)
+        subparser.add_argument("--failures", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_FAILURES)
+        if command == "report":
+            subparser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REPORT)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        data = load_yaml(args.registry)
+        errors, warnings = validate(data, load_schema(args.schema), known_ids(args.deficits, "deficits"), known_ids(args.failures, "failures"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError, yaml.YAMLError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exception:
+        print(f"agent-organ-registry: {exception}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    if args.command == "report":
+        report = build_report(data, args.registry, errors, warnings)
+        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"report={args.output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
+    else:
+        print(f"organs={len(data.get('organs', []))}")
+    for warning in warnings:
+        print(f"warning: {warning}")
+    for error in errors:
+        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
+    print("agent_organ_registry=ok" if not errors else "agent_organ_registry=invalid")
+    return 0 if not errors else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_organ_registry", ROOT / "scripts" / "agent_organ_registry.py")
+assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
+registry_tool = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
+SPEC.loader.exec_module(registry_tool)
+
+
+class AgentOrganRegistryTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
+        cls.schema = registry_tool.load_schema(registry_tool.DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        cls.data = registry_tool.load_yaml(registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        cls.deficits = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_DEFICITS, "deficits")
+        cls.failures = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_FAILURES, "failures")
+
+    def test_actual_registry_validates(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertEqual(errors, [])
+        self.assertEqual(warnings, [])
+
+    def test_unknown_references_are_rejected(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], parent_organs=["ORGAN-999"])]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("unknown parent organ ORGAN-999" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_active_requires_install_trust(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], status="active", trust_level="scene_tested")]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("active requires install_tested" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_report_is_deterministic_and_consumable(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        report = registry_tool.build_report(self.data, registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY, errors, warnings)
+        self.assertTrue(report["valid"])
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["organ_count"], 7)
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_status"], {"active": 1, "candidate": 1, "selected": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_trust_level"], {"install_tested": 2, "scene_tested": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(json.dumps(report))["inventory"][0]["id"], "ORGAN-001")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# Derived registry outputs
+
+These files are reproducible hygiene artifacts, not source-of-truth registry records.
+
+Regenerate `agent_organ_registry_report.json` from the repository root:
+
+```powershell
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py validate
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py report
+```
+
+The report is deterministic for a given source registry: it includes the source SHA-256 and does not include a generation timestamp.

+# Registry contracts
+
+This directory contains executable, machine-readable contracts for Agentum registry surfaces. It preserves the source registries as YAML and does not turn theory or the research archive into runtime payload.
+
+`agent_organ_record.schema.json` encodes the record shape from `03_THEORY/AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC.md`; `scripts/agent_organ_registry.py` adds the checks JSON Schema cannot express cleanly: known relation IDs, duplicate IDs, lifecycle/trust compatibility, and self-lineage prevention.
+
+The current contract deliberately validates only the compact registry lifecycle (`draft`, `candidate`, `selected`, `active`, `quarantined`, `retired`). The richer lifecycle remains governed by `03_THEORY/AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md` until the living registry adopts it.

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate Agentum AgentOrgan records and emit deterministic registry hygiene data."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import yaml
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_SCHEMA = ROOT / "registry_contracts" / "agent_organ_record.schema.json"
+DEFAULT_DEFICITS = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_FAILURES = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_REPORT = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "derived" / "agent_organ_registry_report.json"
+CONTRACT_VERSION = "1.0.0"
+ID_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:ORGAN|DEFICIT|FAILURE)-[0-9]{3,}$")
+TRUST_RANK = {"theory_only": 0, "scene_tested": 1, "install_tested": 2, "field_validated": 3}
+
+
+def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        value = yaml.safe_load(source)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a YAML mapping at document root")
+    return value
+
+
+def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        return json.load(source)
+
+
+def known_ids(path: Path, collection: str) -> set[str]:
+    records = load_yaml(path).get(collection, [])
+    return {record["id"] for record in records if isinstance(record, dict) and isinstance(record.get("id"), str)}
+
+
+def schema_errors(value: Any, rule: dict[str, Any], definitions: dict[str, Any], path: str = "root") -> list[str]:
+    """Validate the JSON-Schema subset used by this checked-in contract.
+
+    Keeping this small interpreter here avoids a second, undeclared validator runtime
+    while making the schema—not a parallel Python field list—the shape authority.
+    """
+    if "$ref" in rule:
+        reference = rule["$ref"]
+        if not reference.startswith("#/$defs/"):
+            return [f"schema {path}: unsupported reference {reference}"]
+        return schema_errors(value, definitions[reference.rsplit("/", 1)[1]], definitions, path)
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    if "enum" in rule and value not in rule["enum"]:
+        errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} is not one of {rule['enum']}")
+    expected = rule.get("type")
+    type_matches = {
+        "object": isinstance(value, dict),
+        "array": isinstance(value, list),
+        "string": isinstance(value, str),
+    }
+    if expected and not type_matches.get(expected, False):
+        return errors + [f"schema {path}: expected {expected}"]
+    if isinstance(value, str):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minLength", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: string is shorter than {rule['minLength']}")
+        if "pattern" in rule and not re.match(rule["pattern"], value):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} does not match {rule['pattern']}")
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minItems", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: requires at least {rule['minItems']} item(s)")
+        if "items" in rule:
+            for index, item in enumerate(value):
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(item, rule["items"], definitions, f"{path}/{index}"))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        properties = rule.get("properties", {})
+        for required in rule.get("required", []):
+            if required not in value:
+                errors.append(f"schema {path}: missing required property {required}")
+        if rule.get("additionalProperties") is False:
+            for key in value:
+                if key not in properties:
+                    errors.append(f"schema {path}: unknown property {key}")
+        for key, child_rule in properties.items():
+            if key in value:
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(value[key], child_rule, definitions, f"{path}/{key}"))
+    return errors
+
+
+def validate(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], deficit_ids: set[str], failure_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    errors.extend(schema_errors(data, schema, schema.get("$defs", {})))
+
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    if not isinstance(organs, list):
+        return errors, warnings
+    organ_ids = [record.get("id") for record in organs if isinstance(record, dict)]
+    duplicates = sorted({item for item, count in Counter(organ_ids).items() if isinstance(item, str) and count > 1})
+    for organ_id in duplicates:
+        errors.append(f"duplicate organ id: {organ_id}")
+
+    known_organs = {item for item in organ_ids if isinstance(item, str)}
+    for index, organ in enumerate(organs):
+        if not isinstance(organ, dict):
+            continue
+        label = organ.get("id", f"organs[{index}]")
+        for field, candidates, known, noun in (
+            ("deficit_origin", organ.get("deficit_origin", []), deficit_ids, "deficit"),
+            ("failure_modes", organ.get("failure_modes", []), failure_ids, "failure mode"),
+            ("parent_organs", organ.get("parent_organs", []), known_organs, "parent organ"),
+            ("child_organs", organ.get("child_organs", []), known_organs, "child organ"),
+        ):
+            if not isinstance(candidates, list):
+                continue
+            for candidate in candidates:
+                if isinstance(candidate, str) and ID_RE.match(candidate) and candidate not in known:
+                    errors.append(f"{label}: {field} references unknown {noun} {candidate}")
+        status, trust = organ.get("status"), organ.get("trust_level")
+        if status in {"selected", "active"} and trust not in {"scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: {status} requires at least scene_tested trust")
+        if status == "active" and trust not in {"install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: active requires install_tested or field_validated trust")
+        if status == "quarantined" and organ.get("quarantine_status") == "none":
+            errors.append(f"{label}: quarantined status requires bounded or strict quarantine_status")
+        if trust == "legacy_only" and status not in {"retired", "quarantined"}:
+            warnings.append(f"{label}: legacy_only trust is normally retired or quarantined")
+        parents, children = organ.get("parent_organs", []), organ.get("child_organs", [])
+        if isinstance(parents, list) and label in parents:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own parent")
+        if isinstance(children, list) and label in children:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own child")
+    return errors, warnings
+
+
+def build_report(data: dict[str, Any], registry_path: Path, errors: list[str], warnings: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    inventory = [
+        {"id": organ["id"], "name": organ["name"], "status": organ["status"], "trust_level": organ["trust_level"], "quarantine_status": organ["quarantine_status"], "output_class": organ["output_class"]}
+        for organ in organs if isinstance(organ, dict) and all(key in organ for key in ("id", "name", "status", "trust_level", "quarantine_status", "output_class"))
+    ]
+    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(registry_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "contract": {"name": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record", "version": CONTRACT_VERSION, "schema": "registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json"},
+        "source": {"path": "02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml", "sha256": source_hash},
+        "valid": not errors,
+        "errors": errors,
+        "warnings": warnings,
+        "summary": {
+            "organ_count": len(inventory),
+            "by_status": dict(sorted(Counter(item["status"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_trust_level": dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_status_and_trust": {
+                status: dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory if item["status"] == status).items()))
+                for status in sorted({item["status"] for item in inventory})
+            },
+        },
+        "inventory": sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: item["id"]),
+    }
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    for command in ("validate", "report"):
+        subparser = subcommands.add_parser(command)
+        subparser.add_argument("--registry", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        subparser.add_argument("--schema", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        subparser.add_argument("--deficits", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DEFICITS)
+        subparser.add_argument("--failures", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_FAILURES)
+        if command == "report":
+            subparser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REPORT)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        data = load_yaml(args.registry)
+        errors, warnings = validate(data, load_schema(args.schema), known_ids(args.deficits, "deficits"), known_ids(args.failures, "failures"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError, yaml.YAMLError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exception:
+        print(f"agent-organ-registry: {exception}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    if args.command == "report":
+        report = build_report(data, args.registry, errors, warnings)
+        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"report={args.output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
+    else:
+        print(f"organs={len(data.get('organs', []))}")
+    for warning in warnings:
+        print(f"warning: {warning}")
+    for error in errors:
+        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
+    print("agent_organ_registry=ok" if not errors else "agent_organ_registry=invalid")
+    return 0 if not errors else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_organ_registry", ROOT / "scripts" / "agent_organ_registry.py")
+assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
+registry_tool = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
+SPEC.loader.exec_module(registry_tool)
+
+
+class AgentOrganRegistryTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
+        cls.schema = registry_tool.load_schema(registry_tool.DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        cls.data = registry_tool.load_yaml(registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        cls.deficits = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_DEFICITS, "deficits")
+        cls.failures = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_FAILURES, "failures")
+
+    def test_actual_registry_validates(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertEqual(errors, [])
+        self.assertEqual(warnings, [])
+
+    def test_unknown_references_are_rejected(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], parent_organs=["ORGAN-999"])]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("unknown parent organ ORGAN-999" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_active_requires_install_trust(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], status="active", trust_level="scene_tested")]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("active requires install_tested" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_report_is_deterministic_and_consumable(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        report = registry_tool.build_report(self.data, registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY, errors, warnings)
+        self.assertTrue(report["valid"])
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["organ_count"], 7)
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_status"], {"active": 1, "candidate": 1, "selected": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_trust_level"], {"install_tested": 2, "scene_tested": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(json.dumps(report))["inventory"][0]["id"], "ORGAN-001")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# Derived registry outputs
+
+These files are reproducible hygiene artifacts, not source-of-truth registry records.
+
+Regenerate `agent_organ_registry_report.json` from the repository root:
+
+```powershell
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py validate
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py report
+```
+
+The report is deterministic for a given source registry: it includes the source SHA-256 and does not include a generation timestamp.

+# Registry contracts
+
+This directory contains executable, machine-readable contracts for Agentum registry surfaces. It preserves the source registries as YAML and does not turn theory or the research archive into runtime payload.
+
+`agent_organ_record.schema.json` encodes the record shape from `03_THEORY/AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC.md`; `scripts/agent_organ_registry.py` adds the checks JSON Schema cannot express cleanly: known relation IDs, duplicate IDs, lifecycle/trust compatibility, and self-lineage prevention.
+
+The current contract deliberately validates only the compact registry lifecycle (`draft`, `candidate`, `selected`, `active`, `quarantined`, `retired`). The richer lifecycle remains governed by `03_THEORY/AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md` until the living registry adopts it.

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate Agentum AgentOrgan records and emit deterministic registry hygiene data."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import yaml
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_SCHEMA = ROOT / "registry_contracts" / "agent_organ_record.schema.json"
+DEFAULT_DEFICITS = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_FAILURES = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_REPORT = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "derived" / "agent_organ_registry_report.json"
+CONTRACT_VERSION = "1.0.0"
+ID_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:ORGAN|DEFICIT|FAILURE)-[0-9]{3,}$")
+TRUST_RANK = {"theory_only": 0, "scene_tested": 1, "install_tested": 2, "field_validated": 3}
+
+
+def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        value = yaml.safe_load(source)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a YAML mapping at document root")
+    return value
+
+
+def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        return json.load(source)
+
+
+def known_ids(path: Path, collection: str) -> set[str]:
+    records = load_yaml(path).get(collection, [])
+    return {record["id"] for record in records if isinstance(record, dict) and isinstance(record.get("id"), str)}
+
+
+def schema_errors(value: Any, rule: dict[str, Any], definitions: dict[str, Any], path: str = "root") -> list[str]:
+    """Validate the JSON-Schema subset used by this checked-in contract.
+
+    Keeping this small interpreter here avoids a second, undeclared validator runtime
+    while making the schema—not a parallel Python field list—the shape authority.
+    """
+    if "$ref" in rule:
+        reference = rule["$ref"]
+        if not reference.startswith("#/$defs/"):
+            return [f"schema {path}: unsupported reference {reference}"]
+        return schema_errors(value, definitions[reference.rsplit("/", 1)[1]], definitions, path)
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    if "enum" in rule and value not in rule["enum"]:
+        errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} is not one of {rule['enum']}")
+    expected = rule.get("type")
+    type_matches = {
+        "object": isinstance(value, dict),
+        "array": isinstance(value, list),
+        "string": isinstance(value, str),
+    }
+    if expected and not type_matches.get(expected, False):
+        return errors + [f"schema {path}: expected {expected}"]
+    if isinstance(value, str):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minLength", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: string is shorter than {rule['minLength']}")
+        if "pattern" in rule and not re.match(rule["pattern"], value):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} does not match {rule['pattern']}")
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minItems", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: requires at least {rule['minItems']} item(s)")
+        if "items" in rule:
+            for index, item in enumerate(value):
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(item, rule["items"], definitions, f"{path}/{index}"))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        properties = rule.get("properties", {})
+        for required in rule.get("required", []):
+            if required not in value:
+                errors.append(f"schema {path}: missing required property {required}")
+        if rule.get("additionalProperties") is False:
+            for key in value:
+                if key not in properties:
+                    errors.append(f"schema {path}: unknown property {key}")
+        for key, child_rule in properties.items():
+            if key in value:
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(value[key], child_rule, definitions, f"{path}/{key}"))
+    return errors
+
+
+def validate(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], deficit_ids: set[str], failure_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    errors.extend(schema_errors(data, schema, schema.get("$defs", {})))
+
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    if not isinstance(organs, list):
+        return errors, warnings
+    organ_ids = [record.get("id") for record in organs if isinstance(record, dict)]
+    duplicates = sorted({item for item, count in Counter(organ_ids).items() if isinstance(item, str) and count > 1})
+    for organ_id in duplicates:
+        errors.append(f"duplicate organ id: {organ_id}")
+
+    known_organs = {item for item in organ_ids if isinstance(item, str)}
+    for index, organ in enumerate(organs):
+        if not isinstance(organ, dict):
+            continue
+        label = organ.get("id", f"organs[{index}]")
+        for field, candidates, known, noun in (
+            ("deficit_origin", organ.get("deficit_origin", []), deficit_ids, "deficit"),
+            ("failure_modes", organ.get("failure_modes", []), failure_ids, "failure mode"),
+            ("parent_organs", organ.get("parent_organs", []), known_organs, "parent organ"),
+            ("child_organs", organ.get("child_organs", []), known_organs, "child organ"),
+        ):
+            if not isinstance(candidates, list):
+                continue
+            for candidate in candidates:
+                if isinstance(candidate, str) and ID_RE.match(candidate) and candidate not in known:
+                    errors.append(f"{label}: {field} references unknown {noun} {candidate}")
+        status, trust = organ.get("status"), organ.get("trust_level")
+        if status in {"selected", "active"} and trust not in {"scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: {status} requires at least scene_tested trust")
+        if status == "active" and trust not in {"install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: active requires install_tested or field_validated trust")
+        if status == "quarantined" and organ.get("quarantine_status") == "none":
+            errors.append(f"{label}: quarantined status requires bounded or strict quarantine_status")
+        if trust == "legacy_only" and status not in {"retired", "quarantined"}:
+            warnings.append(f"{label}: legacy_only trust is normally retired or quarantined")
+        parents, children = organ.get("parent_organs", []), organ.get("child_organs", [])
+        if isinstance(parents, list) and label in parents:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own parent")
+        if isinstance(children, list) and label in children:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own child")
+    return errors, warnings
+
+
+def build_report(data: dict[str, Any], registry_path: Path, errors: list[str], warnings: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    inventory = [
+        {"id": organ["id"], "name": organ["name"], "status": organ["status"], "trust_level": organ["trust_level"], "quarantine_status": organ["quarantine_status"], "output_class": organ["output_class"]}
+        for organ in organs if isinstance(organ, dict) and all(key in organ for key in ("id", "name", "status", "trust_level", "quarantine_status", "output_class"))
+    ]
+    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(registry_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "contract": {"name": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record", "version": CONTRACT_VERSION, "schema": "registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json"},
+        "source": {"path": "02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml", "sha256": source_hash},
+        "valid": not errors,
+        "errors": errors,
+        "warnings": warnings,
+        "summary": {
+            "organ_count": len(inventory),
+            "by_status": dict(sorted(Counter(item["status"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_trust_level": dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_status_and_trust": {
+                status: dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory if item["status"] == status).items()))
+                for status in sorted({item["status"] for item in inventory})
+            },
+        },
+        "inventory": sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: item["id"]),
+    }
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    for command in ("validate", "report"):
+        subparser = subcommands.add_parser(command)
+        subparser.add_argument("--registry", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        subparser.add_argument("--schema", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        subparser.add_argument("--deficits", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DEFICITS)
+        subparser.add_argument("--failures", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_FAILURES)
+        if command == "report":
+            subparser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REPORT)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        data = load_yaml(args.registry)
+        errors, warnings = validate(data, load_schema(args.schema), known_ids(args.deficits, "deficits"), known_ids(args.failures, "failures"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError, yaml.YAMLError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exception:
+        print(f"agent-organ-registry: {exception}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    if args.command == "report":
+        report = build_report(data, args.registry, errors, warnings)
+        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"report={args.output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
+    else:
+        print(f"organs={len(data.get('organs', []))}")
+    for warning in warnings:
+        print(f"warning: {warning}")
+    for error in errors:
+        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
+    print("agent_organ_registry=ok" if not errors else "agent_organ_registry=invalid")
+    return 0 if not errors else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_organ_registry", ROOT / "scripts" / "agent_organ_registry.py")
+assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
+registry_tool = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
+SPEC.loader.exec_module(registry_tool)
+
+
+class AgentOrganRegistryTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
+        cls.schema = registry_tool.load_schema(registry_tool.DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        cls.data = registry_tool.load_yaml(registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        cls.deficits = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_DEFICITS, "deficits")
+        cls.failures = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_FAILURES, "failures")
+
+    def test_actual_registry_validates(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertEqual(errors, [])
+        self.assertEqual(warnings, [])
+
+    def test_unknown_references_are_rejected(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], parent_organs=["ORGAN-999"])]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("unknown parent organ ORGAN-999" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_active_requires_install_trust(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], status="active", trust_level="scene_tested")]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("active requires install_tested" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_report_is_deterministic_and_consumable(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        report = registry_tool.build_report(self.data, registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY, errors, warnings)
+        self.assertTrue(report["valid"])
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["organ_count"], 7)
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_status"], {"active": 1, "candidate": 1, "selected": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_trust_level"], {"install_tested": 2, "scene_tested": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(json.dumps(report))["inventory"][0]["id"], "ORGAN-001")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# Derived registry outputs
+
+These files are reproducible hygiene artifacts, not source-of-truth registry records.
+
+Regenerate `agent_organ_registry_report.json` from the repository root:
+
+```powershell
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py validate
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py report
+```
+
+The report is deterministic for a given source registry: it includes the source SHA-256 and does not include a generation timestamp.

+# Registry contracts
+
+This directory contains executable, machine-readable contracts for Agentum registry surfaces. It preserves the source registries as YAML and does not turn theory or the research archive into runtime payload.
+
+`agent_organ_record.schema.json` encodes the record shape from `03_THEORY/AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC.md`; `scripts/agent_organ_registry.py` adds the checks JSON Schema cannot express cleanly: known relation IDs, duplicate IDs, lifecycle/trust compatibility, and self-lineage prevention.
+
+The current contract deliberately validates only the compact registry lifecycle (`draft`, `candidate`, `selected`, `active`, `quarantined`, `retired`). The richer lifecycle remains governed by `03_THEORY/AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md` until the living registry adopts it.

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c8b8218a41d4c511b2c24693df844586b522ec3c

@@ -0,0 +1,201 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate Agentum AgentOrgan records and emit deterministic registry hygiene data."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import yaml
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_SCHEMA = ROOT / "registry_contracts" / "agent_organ_record.schema.json"
+DEFAULT_DEFICITS = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_FAILURES = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_REPORT = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "derived" / "agent_organ_registry_report.json"
+CONTRACT_VERSION = "1.0.0"
+ID_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:ORGAN|DEFICIT|FAILURE)-[0-9]{3,}$")
+TRUST_RANK = {"theory_only": 0, "scene_tested": 1, "install_tested": 2, "field_validated": 3}
+
+
+def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        value = yaml.safe_load(source)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a YAML mapping at document root")
+    return value
+
+
+def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        return json.load(source)
+
+
+def known_ids(path: Path, collection: str) -> set[str]:
+    records = load_yaml(path).get(collection, [])
+    return {record["id"] for record in records if isinstance(record, dict) and isinstance(record.get("id"), str)}
+
+
+def schema_errors(value: Any, rule: dict[str, Any], definitions: dict[str, Any], path: str = "root") -> list[str]:
+    """Validate the JSON-Schema subset used by this checked-in contract.
+
+    Keeping this small interpreter here avoids a second, undeclared validator runtime
+    while making the schema—not a parallel Python field list—the shape authority.
+    """
+    if "$ref" in rule:
+        reference = rule["$ref"]
+        if not reference.startswith("#/$defs/"):
+            return [f"schema {path}: unsupported reference {reference}"]
+        return schema_errors(value, definitions[reference.rsplit("/", 1)[1]], definitions, path)
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    if "enum" in rule and value not in rule["enum"]:
+        errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} is not one of {rule['enum']}")
+    expected = rule.get("type")
+    type_matches = {
+        "object": isinstance(value, dict),
+        "array": isinstance(value, list),
+        "string": isinstance(value, str),
+    }
+    if expected and not type_matches.get(expected, False):
+        return errors + [f"schema {path}: expected {expected}"]
+    if isinstance(value, str):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minLength", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: string is shorter than {rule['minLength']}")
+        if "pattern" in rule and not re.match(rule["pattern"], value):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} does not match {rule['pattern']}")
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minItems", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: requires at least {rule['minItems']} item(s)")
+        if "items" in rule:
+            for index, item in enumerate(value):
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(item, rule["items"], definitions, f"{path}/{index}"))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        properties = rule.get("properties", {})
+        for required in rule.get("required", []):
+            if required not in value:
+                errors.append(f"schema {path}: missing required property {required}")
+        if rule.get("additionalProperties") is False:
+            for key in value:
+                if key not in properties:
+                    errors.append(f"schema {path}: unknown property {key}")
+        for key, child_rule in properties.items():
+            if key in value:
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(value[key], child_rule, definitions, f"{path}/{key}"))
+    return errors
+
+
+def validate(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], deficit_ids: set[str], failure_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    errors.extend(schema_errors(data, schema, schema.get("$defs", {})))
+
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    if not isinstance(organs, list):
+        return errors, warnings
+    organ_ids = [record.get("id") for record in organs if isinstance(record, dict)]
+    duplicates = sorted({item for item, count in Counter(organ_ids).items() if isinstance(item, str) and count > 1})
+    for organ_id in duplicates:
+        errors.append(f"duplicate organ id: {organ_id}")
+
+    known_organs = {item for item in organ_ids if isinstance(item, str)}
+    for index, organ in enumerate(organs):
+        if not isinstance(organ, dict):
+            continue
+        label = organ.get("id", f"organs[{index}]")
+        for field, candidates, known, noun in (
+            ("deficit_origin", organ.get("deficit_origin", []), deficit_ids, "deficit"),
+            ("failure_modes", organ.get("failure_modes", []), failure_ids, "failure mode"),
+            ("parent_organs", organ.get("parent_organs", []), known_organs, "parent organ"),
+            ("child_organs", organ.get("child_organs", []), known_organs, "child organ"),
+        ):
+            if not isinstance(candidates, list):
+                continue
+            for candidate in candidates:
+                if isinstance(candidate, str) and ID_RE.match(candidate) and candidate not in known:
+                    errors.append(f"{label}: {field} references unknown {noun} {candidate}")
+        status, trust = organ.get("status"), organ.get("trust_level")
+        if status in {"selected", "active"} and trust not in {"scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: {status} requires at least scene_tested trust")
+        if status == "active" and trust not in {"install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: active requires install_tested or field_validated trust")
+        if status == "quarantined" and organ.get("quarantine_status") == "none":
+            errors.append(f"{label}: quarantined status requires bounded or strict quarantine_status")
+        if trust == "legacy_only" and status not in {"retired", "quarantined"}:
+            warnings.append(f"{label}: legacy_only trust is normally retired or quarantined")
+        parents, children = organ.get("parent_organs", []), organ.get("child_organs", [])
+        if isinstance(parents, list) and label in parents:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own parent")
+        if isinstance(children, list) and label in children:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own child")
+    return errors, warnings
+
+
+def build_report(data: dict[str, Any], registry_path: Path, errors: list[str], warnings: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    inventory = [
+        {"id": organ["id"], "name": organ["name"], "status": organ["status"], "trust_level": organ["trust_level"], "quarantine_status": organ["quarantine_status"], "output_class": organ["output_class"]}
+        for organ in organs if isinstance(organ, dict) and all(key in organ for key in ("id", "name", "status", "trust_level", "quarantine_status", "output_class"))
+    ]
+    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(registry_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "contract": {"name": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record", "version": CONTRACT_VERSION, "schema": "registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json"},
+        "source": {"path": "02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml", "sha256": source_hash},
+        "valid": not errors,
+        "errors": errors,
+        "warnings": warnings,
+        "summary": {
+            "organ_count": len(inventory),
+            "by_status": dict(sorted(Counter(item["status"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_trust_level": dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_status_and_trust": {
+                status: dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory if item["status"] == status).items()))
+                for status in sorted({item["status"] for item in inventory})
+            },
+        },
+        "inventory": sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: item["id"]),
+    }
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    for command in ("validate", "report"):
+        subparser = subcommands.add_parser(command)
+        subparser.add_argument("--registry", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        subparser.add_argument("--schema", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        subparser.add_argument("--deficits", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DEFICITS)
+        subparser.add_argument("--failures", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_FAILURES)
+        if command == "report":
+            subparser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REPORT)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        data = load_yaml(args.registry)
+        errors, warnings = validate(data, load_schema(args.schema), known_ids(args.deficits, "deficits"), known_ids(args.failures, "failures"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError, yaml.YAMLError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exception:
+        print(f"agent-organ-registry: {exception}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    if args.command == "report":
+        report = build_report(data, args.registry, errors, warnings)
+        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"report={args.output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
+    else:
+        print(f"organs={len(data.get('organs', []))}")
+    for warning in warnings:
+        print(f"warning: {warning}")
+    for error in errors:
+        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
+    print("agent_organ_registry=ok" if not errors else "agent_organ_registry=invalid")
+    return 0 if not errors else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_organ_registry", ROOT / "scripts" / "agent_organ_registry.py")
+assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
+registry_tool = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
+SPEC.loader.exec_module(registry_tool)
+
+
+class AgentOrganRegistryTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
+        cls.schema = registry_tool.load_schema(registry_tool.DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        cls.data = registry_tool.load_yaml(registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        cls.deficits = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_DEFICITS, "deficits")
+        cls.failures = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_FAILURES, "failures")
+
+    def test_actual_registry_validates(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertEqual(errors, [])
+        self.assertEqual(warnings, [])
+
+    def test_unknown_references_are_rejected(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], parent_organs=["ORGAN-999"])]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("unknown parent organ ORGAN-999" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_active_requires_install_trust(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], status="active", trust_level="scene_tested")]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("active requires install_tested" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_report_is_deterministic_and_consumable(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        report = registry_tool.build_report(self.data, registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY, errors, warnings)
+        self.assertTrue(report["valid"])
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["organ_count"], 7)
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_status"], {"active": 1, "candidate": 1, "selected": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_trust_level"], {"install_tested": 2, "scene_tested": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(json.dumps(report))["inventory"][0]["id"], "ORGAN-001")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# Derived registry outputs
+
+These files are reproducible hygiene artifacts, not source-of-truth registry records.
+
+Regenerate `agent_organ_registry_report.json` from the repository root:
+
+```powershell
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py validate
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py report
+```
+
+The report is deterministic for a given source registry: it includes the source SHA-256 and does not include a generation timestamp.

+# Registry contracts
+
+This directory contains executable, machine-readable contracts for Agentum registry surfaces. It preserves the source registries as YAML and does not turn theory or the research archive into runtime payload.
+
+`agent_organ_record.schema.json` encodes the record shape from `03_THEORY/AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC.md`; `scripts/agent_organ_registry.py` adds the checks JSON Schema cannot express cleanly: known relation IDs, duplicate IDs, lifecycle/trust compatibility, and self-lineage prevention.
+
+The current contract deliberately validates only the compact registry lifecycle (`draft`, `candidate`, `selected`, `active`, `quarantined`, `retired`). The richer lifecycle remains governed by `03_THEORY/AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md` until the living registry adopts it.

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c8b8218a41d4c511b2c24693df844586b522ec3c

@@ -0,0 +1,201 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate Agentum AgentOrgan records and emit deterministic registry hygiene data."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import re
+import sys
+from collections import Counter, defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+import yaml
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+DEFAULT_REGISTRY = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_SCHEMA = ROOT / "registry_contracts" / "agent_organ_record.schema.json"
+DEFAULT_DEFICITS = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "DEFICIT_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_FAILURES = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "FAILURE_MODE_REGISTRY.yaml"
+DEFAULT_REPORT = ROOT / "02_REGISTRIES" / "derived" / "agent_organ_registry_report.json"
+CONTRACT_VERSION = "1.0.0"
+ID_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:ORGAN|DEFICIT|FAILURE)-[0-9]{3,}$")
+TRUST_RANK = {"theory_only": 0, "scene_tested": 1, "install_tested": 2, "field_validated": 3}
+
+
+def load_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        value = yaml.safe_load(source)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a YAML mapping at document root")
+    return value
+
+
+def load_schema(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as source:
+        return json.load(source)
+
+
+def known_ids(path: Path, collection: str) -> set[str]:
+    records = load_yaml(path).get(collection, [])
+    return {record["id"] for record in records if isinstance(record, dict) and isinstance(record.get("id"), str)}
+
+
+def schema_errors(value: Any, rule: dict[str, Any], definitions: dict[str, Any], path: str = "root") -> list[str]:
+    """Validate the JSON-Schema subset used by this checked-in contract.
+
+    Keeping this small interpreter here avoids a second, undeclared validator runtime
+    while making the schema—not a parallel Python field list—the shape authority.
+    """
+    if "$ref" in rule:
+        reference = rule["$ref"]
+        if not reference.startswith("#/$defs/"):
+            return [f"schema {path}: unsupported reference {reference}"]
+        return schema_errors(value, definitions[reference.rsplit("/", 1)[1]], definitions, path)
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    if "enum" in rule and value not in rule["enum"]:
+        errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} is not one of {rule['enum']}")
+    expected = rule.get("type")
+    type_matches = {
+        "object": isinstance(value, dict),
+        "array": isinstance(value, list),
+        "string": isinstance(value, str),
+    }
+    if expected and not type_matches.get(expected, False):
+        return errors + [f"schema {path}: expected {expected}"]
+    if isinstance(value, str):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minLength", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: string is shorter than {rule['minLength']}")
+        if "pattern" in rule and not re.match(rule["pattern"], value):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: {value!r} does not match {rule['pattern']}")
+    if isinstance(value, list):
+        if len(value) < rule.get("minItems", 0):
+            errors.append(f"schema {path}: requires at least {rule['minItems']} item(s)")
+        if "items" in rule:
+            for index, item in enumerate(value):
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(item, rule["items"], definitions, f"{path}/{index}"))
+    if isinstance(value, dict):
+        properties = rule.get("properties", {})
+        for required in rule.get("required", []):
+            if required not in value:
+                errors.append(f"schema {path}: missing required property {required}")
+        if rule.get("additionalProperties") is False:
+            for key in value:
+                if key not in properties:
+                    errors.append(f"schema {path}: unknown property {key}")
+        for key, child_rule in properties.items():
+            if key in value:
+                errors.extend(schema_errors(value[key], child_rule, definitions, f"{path}/{key}"))
+    return errors
+
+
+def validate(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], deficit_ids: set[str], failure_ids: set[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
+    errors: list[str] = []
+    warnings: list[str] = []
+    errors.extend(schema_errors(data, schema, schema.get("$defs", {})))
+
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    if not isinstance(organs, list):
+        return errors, warnings
+    organ_ids = [record.get("id") for record in organs if isinstance(record, dict)]
+    duplicates = sorted({item for item, count in Counter(organ_ids).items() if isinstance(item, str) and count > 1})
+    for organ_id in duplicates:
+        errors.append(f"duplicate organ id: {organ_id}")
+
+    known_organs = {item for item in organ_ids if isinstance(item, str)}
+    for index, organ in enumerate(organs):
+        if not isinstance(organ, dict):
+            continue
+        label = organ.get("id", f"organs[{index}]")
+        for field, candidates, known, noun in (
+            ("deficit_origin", organ.get("deficit_origin", []), deficit_ids, "deficit"),
+            ("failure_modes", organ.get("failure_modes", []), failure_ids, "failure mode"),
+            ("parent_organs", organ.get("parent_organs", []), known_organs, "parent organ"),
+            ("child_organs", organ.get("child_organs", []), known_organs, "child organ"),
+        ):
+            if not isinstance(candidates, list):
+                continue
+            for candidate in candidates:
+                if isinstance(candidate, str) and ID_RE.match(candidate) and candidate not in known:
+                    errors.append(f"{label}: {field} references unknown {noun} {candidate}")
+        status, trust = organ.get("status"), organ.get("trust_level")
+        if status in {"selected", "active"} and trust not in {"scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: {status} requires at least scene_tested trust")
+        if status == "active" and trust not in {"install_tested", "field_validated"}:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: active requires install_tested or field_validated trust")
+        if status == "quarantined" and organ.get("quarantine_status") == "none":
+            errors.append(f"{label}: quarantined status requires bounded or strict quarantine_status")
+        if trust == "legacy_only" and status not in {"retired", "quarantined"}:
+            warnings.append(f"{label}: legacy_only trust is normally retired or quarantined")
+        parents, children = organ.get("parent_organs", []), organ.get("child_organs", [])
+        if isinstance(parents, list) and label in parents:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own parent")
+        if isinstance(children, list) and label in children:
+            errors.append(f"{label}: cannot be its own child")
+    return errors, warnings
+
+
+def build_report(data: dict[str, Any], registry_path: Path, errors: list[str], warnings: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    organs = data.get("organs", [])
+    inventory = [
+        {"id": organ["id"], "name": organ["name"], "status": organ["status"], "trust_level": organ["trust_level"], "quarantine_status": organ["quarantine_status"], "output_class": organ["output_class"]}
+        for organ in organs if isinstance(organ, dict) and all(key in organ for key in ("id", "name", "status", "trust_level", "quarantine_status", "output_class"))
+    ]
+    source_hash = hashlib.sha256(registry_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+    return {
+        "contract": {"name": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record", "version": CONTRACT_VERSION, "schema": "registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json"},
+        "source": {"path": "02_REGISTRIES/AGENT_ORGAN_REGISTRY.yaml", "sha256": source_hash},
+        "valid": not errors,
+        "errors": errors,
+        "warnings": warnings,
+        "summary": {
+            "organ_count": len(inventory),
+            "by_status": dict(sorted(Counter(item["status"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_trust_level": dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory).items())),
+            "by_status_and_trust": {
+                status: dict(sorted(Counter(item["trust_level"] for item in inventory if item["status"] == status).items()))
+                for status in sorted({item["status"] for item in inventory})
+            },
+        },
+        "inventory": sorted(inventory, key=lambda item: item["id"]),
+    }
+
+
+def main() -> int:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    for command in ("validate", "report"):
+        subparser = subcommands.add_parser(command)
+        subparser.add_argument("--registry", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        subparser.add_argument("--schema", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        subparser.add_argument("--deficits", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DEFICITS)
+        subparser.add_argument("--failures", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_FAILURES)
+        if command == "report":
+            subparser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_REPORT)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+    try:
+        data = load_yaml(args.registry)
+        errors, warnings = validate(data, load_schema(args.schema), known_ids(args.deficits, "deficits"), known_ids(args.failures, "failures"))
+    except (OSError, ValueError, yaml.YAMLError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exception:
+        print(f"agent-organ-registry: {exception}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 2
+    if args.command == "report":
+        report = build_report(data, args.registry, errors, warnings)
+        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print(f"report={args.output.relative_to(ROOT)}")
+    else:
+        print(f"organs={len(data.get('organs', []))}")
+    for warning in warnings:
+        print(f"warning: {warning}")
+    for error in errors:
+        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
+    print("agent_organ_registry=ok" if not errors else "agent_organ_registry=invalid")
+    return 0 if not errors else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_organ_registry", ROOT / "scripts" / "agent_organ_registry.py")
+assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
+registry_tool = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
+SPEC.loader.exec_module(registry_tool)
+
+
+class AgentOrganRegistryTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    @classmethod
+    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
+        cls.schema = registry_tool.load_schema(registry_tool.DEFAULT_SCHEMA)
+        cls.data = registry_tool.load_yaml(registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
+        cls.deficits = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_DEFICITS, "deficits")
+        cls.failures = registry_tool.known_ids(registry_tool.DEFAULT_FAILURES, "failures")
+
+    def test_actual_registry_validates(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertEqual(errors, [])
+        self.assertEqual(warnings, [])
+
+    def test_unknown_references_are_rejected(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], parent_organs=["ORGAN-999"])]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("unknown parent organ ORGAN-999" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_active_requires_install_trust(self) -> None:
+        data = {"organs": [dict(self.data["organs"][0], status="active", trust_level="scene_tested")]}
+        errors, _ = registry_tool.validate(data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        self.assertTrue(any("active requires install_tested" in error for error in errors))
+
+    def test_report_is_deterministic_and_consumable(self) -> None:
+        errors, warnings = registry_tool.validate(self.data, self.schema, self.deficits, self.failures)
+        report = registry_tool.build_report(self.data, registry_tool.DEFAULT_REGISTRY, errors, warnings)
+        self.assertTrue(report["valid"])
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["organ_count"], 7)
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_status"], {"active": 1, "candidate": 1, "selected": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(report["summary"]["by_trust_level"], {"install_tested": 2, "scene_tested": 5})
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(json.dumps(report))["inventory"][0]["id"], "ORGAN-001")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+# Derived registry outputs
+
+These files are reproducible hygiene artifacts, not source-of-truth registry records.
+
+Regenerate `agent_organ_registry_report.json` from the repository root:
+
+```powershell
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py validate
+python scripts/agent_organ_registry.py report
+```
+
+The report is deterministic for a given source registry: it includes the source SHA-256 and does not include a generation timestamp.

+# Registry contracts
+
+This directory contains executable, machine-readable contracts for Agentum registry surfaces. It preserves the source registries as YAML and does not turn theory or the research archive into runtime payload.
+
+`agent_organ_record.schema.json` encodes the record shape from `03_THEORY/AGENT_ORGAN_RECORD_SPEC.md`; `scripts/agent_organ_registry.py` adds the checks JSON Schema cannot express cleanly: known relation IDs, duplicate IDs, lifecycle/trust compatibility, and self-lineage prevention.
+
+The current contract deliberately validates only the compact registry lifecycle (`draft`, `candidate`, `selected`, `active`, `quarantined`, `retired`). The richer lifecycle remains governed by `03_THEORY/AGENTUM_ORGAN_TRUST_AND_LIFECYCLE.md` until the living registry adopts it.
diff --git a/registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json b/registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..bdd33e1b8f6ffface3c975a57e247d79dc51c26a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/registry_contracts/agent_organ_record.schema.json
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+{
+  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
+  "$id": "https://agentum.local/schemas/agent-organ-record/v1",
+  "title": "Agentum AgentOrgan Record",
+  "description": "Machine-readable contract for durable AgentOrgan registry records. Semantic link and lifecycle checks are implemented by scripts/agent_organ_registry.py.",
+  "type": "object",
+  "required": ["organs"],
+  "additionalProperties": false,
+  "properties": {
+    "organs": {
+      "type": "array",
+      "minItems": 1,
+      "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/organ" }
+    }
+  },
+  "$defs": {
+    "nonEmptyString": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1 },
+    "stringList": {
+      "type": "array",
+      "items": { "$ref": "#/$defs/nonEmptyString" }
+    },
+    "organ": {
+      "type": "object",
+      "additionalProperties": false,
+      "required": [
+        "id", "name", "status", "version", "trust_level", "project_scope",
+        "deficit_origin", "function", "non_function", "working_object", "output_class",
+        "input_contract", "output_contract", "context_requirements", "memory_dependencies",
+        "memory_regimes_used", "memory_authority_limits", "tools_allowed", "write_surface",
+        "approval_boundary", "runtime_risks", "test_scenes", "evaluation_criteria", "failure_modes",
+        "critic_links", "lineage", "parent_organs", "child_organs", "mutation_history",
+        "activation_policy", "activation_levels", "observability_hooks", "provenance_policy",
+        "quarantine_status", "install_scope"
+      ],
+      "properties": {
+        "id": { "type": "string", "pattern": "^ORGAN-[0-9]{3,}$" },
+        "name": { "$ref": "#/$defs/nonEmptyString" },
+        "status": { "enum": ["draft", "candidate", "selected", "active", "quarantined", "retired"] },
+        "version": { "$ref": "#/$defs/nonEmptyString" },
+        "trust_level": { "enum": ["theory_only", "scene_tested", "install_tested", "field_validated", "legacy_only"] },
+        "project_scope": { "$ref": "#/$defs/stringList", "minItems": 1 },
+        "deficit_origin": { "$ref": "#/$defs/stringList", "minItems": 1 },
+        "function": { "$ref": "#/$defs/nonEmptyString" },
+        "non_function": { "$ref": "#/$defs/stringList", "minItems": 1 },
+        "working_object": { "$ref": "#/$defs/stringList", "minItems": 1 },

diff --git a/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py b/scripts/agent_organ_registry.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c8b8218a41d4c511b2c24693df844586b522ec3c
